666:-- 10) Service-role RPC: kiÅŸisel ilan giriÅŸi (AC-3, AC-5, AC-6, AC-11, AC-S3, AC-S4).
667:--     owner ASLA p_record'dan okunmaz; p_agent_id sidecar oturumunun sahibidir.
668:--     SeÃ§im otoritesi DB'dir (AC-5, AC-9): source_group bu agent'Ä±n wa_group_selection
669:--     kaydÄ±nda yoksa (seÃ§im kaldÄ±rÄ±lmÄ±ÅŸ / hiÃ§ seÃ§ilmemiÅŸ) hiÃ§bir ÅŸey yazÄ±lmaz -> 'unselected'.
670:--     DÃ¶nÃ¼ÅŸ: 'approved' | 'pending' | 'duplicate' | 'unselected'. Tek transaction: hata -> tÃ¼mÃ¼ geri alÄ±nÄ±r.
671:--     SonuÃ§ ve eÅŸik fallback'i sayaÃ§lara bu fonksiyon iÃ§inde, aynÄ± transaction'da yazÄ±lÄ±r.
672:create or replace function ingest_personal_listing(p_agent_id uuid, p_record jsonb)
673:returns text
674:language plpgsql
675:security definer
676:set search_path = public
677:as $$
678:declare
679:    v_value jsonb;
680:    v_threshold numeric := 0.70;
681:    v_fallback boolean := false;
682:    v_score numeric;
683:    v_approve boolean;
684:    v_parsed jsonb;
685:    v_raw_id uuid;
686:begin
687:    if p_agent_id is null or p_record is null or jsonb_typeof(p_record) <> 'object' then
688:        raise exception 'invalid_record';
689:    end if;
690:
691:    -- SeÃ§im kontrolÃ¼ (yazmadan Ã¶nce). source_group null ise eÅŸleÅŸme olmaz -> unselected.
692:    if not exists (
693:        select 1 from wa_group_selection ws
694:        where ws.agent_id = p_agent_id
695:          and ws.group_jid = p_record->>'source_group'
696:    ) then
697:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_unselected');
698:        return 'unselected';
699:    end if;
700:
701:    -- EÅŸik: satÄ±r yoksa / okunamazsa / geÃ§ersizse varsayÄ±lan 0.70 (threshold_fallback).
702:    select s.value into v_value
703:    from app_settings s
704:    where s.key = 'personal_auto_approve_threshold';
705:
706:    if not found then
707:        v_fallback := true;
708:    else
709:        begin
710:            v_threshold := (v_value #>> '{}')::numeric;
711:        exception when others then
712:            v_threshold := null;
713:        end;
714:    end if;
715:
716:    if v_threshold is null or v_threshold < 0 or v_threshold > 1 then
717:        v_threshold := 0.70;
718:        v_fallback := true;
719:    end if;
720:
721:    if v_fallback then
722:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('threshold_fallback');
723:    end if;
724:
725:    -- Skor: null / sayÄ± deÄŸil / 0-1 dÄ±ÅŸÄ± -> pending (kuyruk).
726:    v_score := case
727:        when p_record->>'quality_score' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (p_record->>'quality_score')::numeric
728:    end;
729:    if v_score is not null and (v_score < 0 or v_score > 1) then
730:        v_score := null;
731:    end if;
732:
733:    v_approve := v_score is not null and v_score >= v_threshold;
734:    v_parsed := p_record->'parsed';
735:
736:    begin
737:        insert into raw_listings (
738:            source, source_group, source_message_id, raw_text, parsed, fingerprint,
739:            quality_score, status, owner_agent_id, source_group_name, reviewed_at
740:        )
741:        values (
742:            'whatsapp_personal',
743:            p_record->>'source_group',
744:            case when p_record->>'source_message_id' is not null
745:                 then p_agent_id::text || ':' || (p_record->>'source_message_id')
746:            end,
747:            p_record->>'raw_text',
748:            v_parsed,
749:            p_record->>'fingerprint',
750:            v_score,
751:            case when v_approve then 'approved' else 'pending' end,
752:            p_agent_id,
753:            p_record->>'source_group_name',
754:            case when v_approve then now() end
755:        )
756:        returning id into v_raw_id;
757:    exception when unique_violation then
758:        -- AynÄ± emlakÃ§Ä±da aynÄ± fingerprint/mesaj: tek kayÄ±t kalÄ±r, yazma yok (AC-11).
759:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_duplicate');
760:        return 'duplicate';
761:    end;
762:
763:    if v_approve then
764:        insert into listings (
765:            raw_listing_id, title, price, currency, rooms, area_m2, city, district,
766:            neighborhood, description, source_group_name, owner_agent_id
767:        )
768:        values (
769:            v_raw_id,
770:            coalesce(v_parsed->>'title', trim(concat(v_parsed->>'rooms', ' ', v_parsed->>'district', ' ', v_parsed->>'city'))),
771:            (v_parsed->>'price')::numeric,
772:            coalesce(v_parsed->>'currency', 'TRY'),
773:            v_parsed->>'rooms',
774:            (v_parsed->>'area_m2')::numeric,
775:            v_parsed->>'city',
776:            v_parsed->>'district',
777:            v_parsed->>'neighborhood',
778:            v_parsed->>'description',
779:            p_record->>'source_group_name',
780:            p_agent_id
781:        );
782:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_auto_approved');
783:        return 'approved';
784:    end if;
785:
786:    perform bump_sidecar_counter(
787:        case when v_score is null then 'personal_queued_noscore' else 'personal_queued' end
788:    );
789:    return 'pending';
790:end;
791:$$;
792:
793:revoke all on function ingest_personal_listing(uuid, jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated;

41:function backoffDelay(retries, rnd = Math.random) {
42:  const base = Math.min(BACKOFF_MAX_MS, BACKOFF_BASE_MS * 2 ** retries);
43:  const jitter = 0.9 + rnd() * 0.2; // Â±%10
44:  return Math.min(BACKOFF_MAX_MS, Math.round(base * jitter));
45:}

65:class SessionManager {
66:  constructor({
67:    rest,
68:    makeSocket,
69:    authRoot,
70:    key,
71:    maxSessions = DEFAULT_MAX_SESSIONS,
72:    now = Date.now,
73:    setTimeout: schedule = globalThis.setTimeout,
74:    clearTimeout: unschedule = globalThis.clearTimeout,
75:    rnd = Math.random,
76:    webhook = null,
77:  }) {
78:    this.rest = rest;
79:    this.makeSocket = makeSocket;
80:    this.authRoot = authRoot;
81:    this.key = key || null;
82:    this.maxSessions = Number.isInteger(maxSessions) && maxSessions > 0 ? maxSessions : DEFAULT_MAX_SESSIONS;
83:    this.now = now;
84:    this._schedule = schedule;
85:    this._unschedule = unschedule;
86:    this.rnd = rnd;
87:    // { url, secret, fetchImpl }: yalnÄ±z seÃ§ili grup ilanlarÄ± buraya POST edilir.
88:    this.webhook = webhook;
89:
90:    this.sessions = new Map();
91:    this.running = false;
92:    this._ticking = false;
93:    this.counters = { ticks: 0, reconnects: 0, terminated: 0 };
94:    this.msgCounters = createCounters();
95:  }
96:
97:  _iso(ms) {
98:    return new Date(ms).toISOString();
99:  }
100:
101:  async _guard(label, fn) {
102:    try {
103:      await fn();
104:    } catch (e) {
105:      logError(`${label} hata`, { error: (e && (e.code || e.name)) || 'error' });
106:    }
107:  }
108:
109:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
110:  // YaÅŸam dÃ¶ngÃ¼sÃ¼
111:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
112:
113:  async start() {
114:    if (this.running) return;
115:    this.running = true;
116:
117:    if (!this.key) {
118:      logError('WA_AUTH_KEY tanÄ±mlÄ± deÄŸil; kiÅŸisel baÄŸlantÄ±lar sidecar_misconfigured ile reddedilir');
119:    }
120:
121:    await this._guard('heartbeat', () => this._beat());
122:
123:    let ids = [];
124:    if (this.key) {
125:      try {
126:        ids = await this.rest.listConnectedAgents();
127:      } catch (e) {
128:        logError('baÄŸlÄ± agent listesi alÄ±namadÄ±', { error: e.name || 'error' });
129:      }
130:    }
131:    // AÃ§Ä±lÄ±ÅŸta baÄŸlÄ± oturumlar 2 sn arayla yeniden aÃ§Ä±lÄ±r (AC-8).
132:    ids.forEach((id, i) => {
133:      this._schedule(() => this._guard('resume', () => this._resume(id)), i * RESUME_GAP_MS);
134:    });
135:
136:    this._loop(POLL_MS, () => this.tick());
137:    this._loop(NO_QR_SWEEP_MS, () => this._sweepNoQr());
138:    this._loop(HEARTBEAT_MS, () => this._beat());
139:    this._loop(SUBSCRIPTION_CHECK_MS, () => this._checkSubscriptions());
140:    this._loop(GROUP_SYNC_MS, () => this._syncAllGroups());
141:    this._loop(SELECTION_TTL_MS, () => this._refreshAllSelections());
142:  }
143:
144:  _loop(ms, fn) {
145:    const run = async () => {
146:      if (!this.running) return;
147:      await this._guard('dÃ¶ngÃ¼', fn);
148:      if (this.running) this._schedule(run, ms);
149:    };
150:    this._schedule(run, ms);
151:  }
152:
153:  stop() {
154:    this.running = false;
155:    for (const s of this.sessions.values()) {
156:      if (s.sock) {
157:        try {
158:          if (typeof s.sock.end === 'function') s.sock.end(undefined);
159:        } catch (_) {
160:          // yoksay
161:        }
162:      }
163:    }
164:    this.sessions.clear();
165:  }
166:
167:  async _beat() {
168:    await this.rest.heartbeat({
169:      sessions_open: this.sessions.size,
170:      misconfigured: !this.key,
171:      counters: { ...this.counters, ...this.msgCounters },
172:    });
173:  }
174:
175:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
176:  // Ä°stek poll'u
177:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
178:
179:  async tick() {
180:    if (this._ticking) return;
181:    this._ticking = true;
182:    this.counters.ticks += 1;
183:    try {
184:      const rows = await this.rest.fetchPendingRequests();
185:      for (const row of rows) {
186:        await this._guard('istek', () => this._handle(row));
187:      }
188:    } finally {
189:      this._ticking = false;
190:    }
191:  }
192:
193:  async _handle(row) {
194:    const agentId = row && row.agent_id;
195:    const attemptId = (row && row.attempt_id) || null;
196:    if (!isValidAgentId(agentId)) {
197:      logError('geÃ§ersiz agent_id iÃ§eren istek atlandÄ±');
198:      return;
199:    }
200:    if (row.requested_action === 'connect') return this._connect(agentId, attemptId);
201:    if (row.requested_action === 'disconnect') return this._disconnect(agentId, attemptId);
202:    return this.rest.update(agentId, { requested_action: null });
203:  }
204:
205:  async _connect(agentId, attemptId) {
206:    if (!this.key) {
207:      return this._failRequest(agentId, attemptId, 'sidecar_misconfigured');
208:    }
209:    if (this.sessions.has(agentId)) {
210:      return this.rest.update(agentId, { requested_action: null });
211:    }
212:    if (this.sessions.size >= this.maxSessions) {
213:      return this._failRequest(agentId, attemptId, 'capacity');
214:    }
215:
216:    // Yeni eÅŸleÅŸtirme temiz baÅŸlar: eski kalÄ±ntÄ± creds ile giriÅŸ denenmez.
217:    removeAgentAuth(this.authRoot, agentId);
218:    const s = {
219:      agentId,
220:      attemptId,
221:      sock: null,
222:      status: 'pending',
223:      qrCount: 0,
224:      opened: false,
225:      retries: 0,
226:      fresh: true,
227:    };
228:    this.sessions.set(agentId, s);
229:    await this.rest.update(agentId, { requested_action: null, last_seen_at: this._iso(this.now()) });
230:    log('baÄŸlantÄ± baÅŸlatÄ±ldÄ±', { agent_id: agentId });
231:    await this._startOrFail(s);
232:  }
233:
234:  async _failRequest(agentId, attemptId, errorCode) {
235:    await this.rest.update(agentId, {
236:      status: 'error',
237:      error_code: errorCode,
238:      requested_action: null,
239:      last_seen_at: this._iso(this.now()),
240:    });
241:    await this.rest.insertEvent(agentId, {
242:      attempt_id: attemptId,
243:      from_status: null,
244:      to_status: 'error',
245:      error_code: errorCode,
246:    });
247:    logError('istek reddedildi', { agent_id: agentId, error_code: errorCode });
248:  }
249:
250:  async _disconnect(agentId, attemptId) {
251:    const s = this.sessions.get(agentId);
252:    const from = s ? s.status : null;
253:    if (s) {
254:      s.terminated = true;
255:      this.sessions.delete(agentId);
256:      const sock = s.sock;
257:      s.sock = null;
258:      if (sock) closeSocket(sock);
259:    }
260:    removeAgentAuth(this.authRoot, agentId);
261:    await this.rest.deleteConnection(agentId);
262:    await this.rest.insertEvent(agentId, {
263:      attempt_id: attemptId,
264:      from_status: from,
265:      to_status: 'disconnected',
266:      error_code: null,
267:    });
268:    log('baÄŸlantÄ± kaldÄ±rÄ±ldÄ±', { agent_id: agentId });
269:  }
270:
271:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
272:  // Soket aÃ§ma
273:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
274:
275:  async _resume(agentId) {
276:    if (!this.key || this.sessions.has(agentId)) return;
277:    if (this.sessions.size >= this.maxSessions) {
278:      logError('kapasite dolu, aÃ§Ä±lÄ±ÅŸta oturum atlandÄ±', { agent_id: agentId });
279:      return;
280:    }
281:    // Slot await'ten Ã–NCE senkron rezerve edilir: bu sÄ±rada tick()->_connect aynÄ± agent iÃ§in
282:    // ikinci soket aÃ§amaz (_connect sessions.has ile erken dÃ¶ner).
283:    const s = this._newSession(agentId, null, 'connected', false);
284:    this.sessions.set(agentId, s);
285:
286:    let active;
287:    try {
288:      active = await this.rest.isSubscriptionActive(agentId);
289:    } catch (e) {
290:      this._release(s);
291:      throw e;
292:    }
293:    // Bekleme sÄ±rasÄ±nda oturum sonlandÄ±rÄ±ldÄ±/kaldÄ±rÄ±ldÄ± ise devam etme.
294:    if (this.sessions.get(agentId) !== s) return;
295:
296:    if (!active) {
297:      return this._terminate(
298:        s,
299:        { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'subscription_inactive', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
300:        { logout: false, deleteAuth: true },
301:      );
302:    }
303:    await this._startOrFail(s);
304:  }
305:
306:  // Rezervasyonu (henÃ¼z soket aÃ§Ä±lmamÄ±ÅŸ oturumu) geri al.
307:  _release(s) {
308:    if (this.sessions.get(s.agentId) === s) this.sessions.delete(s.agentId);
309:  }
310:
311:  _newSession(agentId, attemptId, status, fresh) {
312:    return { agentId, attemptId, sock: null, status, qrCount: 0, opened: false, retries: 0, fresh };
313:  }
314:
315:  async _startOrFail(s) {
316:    try {
317:      await this._spawn(s, s.fresh);
318:    } catch (e) {
319:      logError('oturum aÃ§Ä±lamadÄ±', { agent_id: s.agentId, error: e.name || 'error' });
320:      await this._terminate(
321:        s,
322:        { status: 'error', error_code: 'auth_load_failed', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
323:        { logout: false, deleteAuth: true },
324:      );
325:    }
326:  }
327:
328:  async _spawn(s, fresh) {
329:    const { state, saveCreds } = await useEncryptedAuthState(
330:      agentAuthDir(this.authRoot, s.agentId),
331:      this.key,
332:    );
333:    // Auth okunurken oturum iptal edildiyse soket aÃ§Ä±lmaz.
334:    if (this.sessions.get(s.agentId) !== s) return;
335:
336:    // AynÄ± oturumda canlÄ± bir soket varsa yenisini aÃ§madan Ã¶nce kapat (sÄ±zÄ±ntÄ± olmasÄ±n).
337:    if (s.sock) {
338:      closeSocket(s.sock);
339:      s.sock = null;
340:    }
341:
342:    const sock = this.makeSocket({
343:      agentId: s.agentId,
344:      auth: state,
345:      syncFullHistory: false,
346:      markOnlineOnConnect: false,
347:    });
348:    s.sock = sock;
349:    s.fresh = fresh;
350:    s.startedAt = this.now();
351:
352:    sock.ev.on('connection.update', (u) => this._guard('baÄŸlantÄ± olayÄ±', () => this._onUpdate(s, sock, u)));
353:    sock.ev.on('creds.update', () => this._guard('creds olayÄ±', () => this._onCreds(s, sock, saveCreds)));
354:    sock.ev.on('groups.update', () => this._guard('grup gÃ¼ncelleme', () => this._syncGroups(s, sock)));
355:  }
356:
357:  _isCurrent(s, sock) {
358:    return this.sessions.get(s.agentId) === s && s.sock === sock;
359:  }
360:
361:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
362:  // Baileys olaylarÄ±
363:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
364:
365:  async _onUpdate(s, sock, u) {
366:    if (!this._isCurrent(s, sock)) return;
367:    const { connection, lastDisconnect, qr } = u || {};
368:    if (qr) return this._onQr(s, qr);
369:    if (connection === 'open') return this._onOpen(s, sock);
370:    if (connection === 'close') {
371:      return this._onClose(s, sock, lastDisconnect && lastDisconnect.error && lastDisconnect.error.output
372:        ? lastDisconnect.error.output.statusCode
373:        : undefined);
374:    }
375:    return undefined;
376:  }
377:
378:  async _onQr(s, qr) {
379:    s.qrCount += 1;
380:    if (s.qrCount > QR_MAX_SHOWS) {
381:      return this._terminate(
382:        s,
383:        { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'qr_expired', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
384:        { logout: true, deleteAuth: true },
385:      );
386:    }
387:    const prev = s.status;
388:    s.status = 'qr';
389:    await this.rest.update(s.agentId, {
390:      status: 'qr',
391:      qr,
392:      qr_expires_at: this._iso(this.now() + QR_TTL_MS),
393:      qr_count: s.qrCount,
394:      error_code: null,
395:    });
396:    if (prev !== 'qr') {
397:      await this.rest.insertEvent(s.agentId, {
398:        attempt_id: s.attemptId,
399:        from_status: prev,
400:        to_status: 'qr',
401:        error_code: null,
402:      });
403:    }
404:  }
405:
406:  async _onOpen(s, sock) {
407:    s.opened = true;
408:    s.fresh = false;
409:    s.retries = 0;
410:    const prev = s.status;
411:    s.status = 'connected';
412:    await this.rest.update(s.agentId, {
413:      status: 'connected',
414:      phone_masked: maskPhone(sock.user && sock.user.id),
415:      qr: null,
416:      qr_expires_at: null,
417:      error_code: null,
418:      last_seen_at: this._iso(this.now()),
419:    });
420:    await this.rest.insertEvent(s.agentId, {
421:      attempt_id: s.attemptId,
422:      from_status: prev,
423:      to_status: 'connected',
424:      error_code: null,
425:    });
426:    log('baÄŸlandÄ±', { agent_id: s.agentId });
427:    // EML-107: dinleyici baÄŸlÄ±yken HER gelen mesaj sÄ±nÄ±flandÄ±rÄ±lÄ±r (seÃ§im boÅŸ olsa bile); seÃ§ili
428:    // olmayan grup/DM yalnÄ±z sayaÃ§ artÄ±rÄ±r. SeÃ§im yokluÄŸu dinleyiciyi kapatmaz.
429:    this._attachMessages(s, sock);
430:    await this._guard('grup senkronu', () => this._syncGroups(s, sock));
431:  }
432:
433:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
434:  // Gruplar ve seÃ§im (EML-107 AC-1, AC-S7)
435:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
436:
437:  // wa_groups upsert (hesap baÅŸÄ±na en fazla MAX_GROUPS); ardÄ±ndan seÃ§im yenilenir.
438:  async _syncGroups(s, sock) {
439:    if (!this._isCurrent(s, sock) || typeof sock.groupFetchAllParticipating !== 'function') return;
440:    const meta = await sock.groupFetchAllParticipating();
441:    const capped = capGroups(Object.values(meta || {}), MAX_GROUPS);
442:    if (capped.truncated) bump(this.msgCounters, 'groups_truncated');
443:    const rows = toGroupRows(s.agentId, capped.groups);
444:    s.groupNames = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.group_jid, r.name]));
445:    if (rows.length > 0) await this.rest.upsertWaGroups(s.agentId, rows);
446:    await this._refreshSelection(s);
447:  }
448:
449:  async _syncAllGroups() {
450:    for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {
451:      if (s.opened && s.sock) await this._guard('grup periyodik', () => this._syncGroups(s, s.sock));
452:    }
453:  }
454:
455:  // SeÃ§im Ã¶nbelleÄŸi (60 sn). Okuma hatasÄ±nda boÅŸ kÃ¼me: hiÃ§bir grup iÅŸlenmez (pesimist).
456:  async _refreshSelection(s) {
457:    let set;
458:    try {
459:      const jids = await this.rest.fetchSelection(s.agentId);
460:      set = new Set(Array.isArray(jids) ? jids : []);
461:    } catch (e) {
462:      bump(this.msgCounters, 'selection_read_error');
463:      logError('grup seÃ§imi okunamadÄ±', { agent_id: s.agentId, error: (e && e.name) || 'error' });
464:      set = new Set();
465:    }
466:    s.selection = { set, at: this.now() };
467:    return set;
468:  }
469:
470:  async _refreshAllSelections() {
471:    for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {
472:      if (s.opened && s.sock) await this._guard('seÃ§im yenileme', () => this._refreshSelection(s));
473:    }
474:  }
475:
476:  async _selectionOf(s) {
477:    if (!s.selection || this.now() - s.selection.at >= SELECTION_TTL_MS) await this._refreshSelection(s);
478:    return s.selection ? s.selection.set : new Set();
479:  }
480:
481:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
482:  // Mesaj iÅŸleme (yalnÄ±z seÃ§ili gruplar; DM ve seÃ§ilmeyenler iÃ§eriksiz sayaÃ§la dÃ¼ÅŸer)
483:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
484:
485:  // Dinleyici oturum soketine en fazla bir kez baÄŸlanÄ±r (yeniden baÄŸlanmada yeni soket iÃ§in tekrar).
486:  _attachMessages(s, sock) {
487:    if (s.msgSock === sock) return;
488:    s.msgSock = sock;
489:    sock.ev.on('messages.upsert', (u) => this._guard('mesaj olayÄ±', () => this._onMessages(s, sock, u)));
490:  }
491:
492:  async _onMessages(s, sock, u) {
493:    if (!this._isCurrent(s, sock)) return;
494:    const { messages, type } = u || {};
495:    if (type !== 'notify' || !Array.isArray(messages)) return;
496:    for (const msg of messages) {
497:      await this._guard('mesaj', () => this._onMessage(s, msg));
498:    }
499:  }
500:
501:  async _onMessage(s, msg) {
502:    // Kendi gÃ¶nderdiklerimiz gelen mesaj deÄŸildir.
503:    if (!msg || !msg.key || msg.key.fromMe) return;
504:
505:    // SÄ±nÄ±flandÄ±rma metin Ã§Ä±karmadan Ã–NCE: seÃ§ili olmayan grup/DM iÃ§eriÄŸi hiÃ§ okunmaz (AC-S4).
506:    const remoteJid = msg.key.remoteJid;
507:    const selection = await this._selectionOf(s);
508:    const outcome = classifyIncoming({ remoteJid }, selection);
509:    if (outcome !== 'process') {
510:      bump(this.msgCounters, outcome);
511:      return;
512:    }
513:
514:    // SeÃ§ili grup + metinsiz (tepki, protokol) mesaj: ilan adayÄ± deÄŸildir, sayÄ±lmaz.
515:    const text = messageText(msg.message);
516:    if (!text) return;
517:
518:    const groupName = (s.groupNames && s.groupNames.get(remoteJid)) || null;
519:    const payload = { id: msg.key.id, text, group: remoteJid, groupName, agentId: s.agentId };
520:    const ok = await this._postWebhook(payload);
521:    if (!ok) bump(this.msgCounters, 'webhook_post_failed');
522:  }
523:
524:  // Ortak kÃ¶prÃ¼nÃ¼n (bridge.js) webhook desenine uyumlu: 3 deneme, bekleme yok. Ä°Ã§erik loglanmaz.
525:  async _postWebhook(payload) {
526:    const { url, secret, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch } = this.webhook || {};
527:    if (!url || !secret) return false;
528:    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= WEBHOOK_MAX_ATTEMPTS; attempt += 1) {
529:      try {
530:        const res = await fetchImpl(url, {
531:          method: 'POST',
532:          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${secret}` },
533:          body: JSON.stringify(payload),
534:        });
535:        if (res.ok) return true;
536:        logError('webhook yanÄ±tÄ± baÅŸarÄ±sÄ±z', { status: res.status });
537:      } catch (e) {
538:        logError('webhook baÄŸlantÄ± hatasÄ±', { error: (e && e.name) || 'error' });
539:      }
540:    }
541:    return false;
542:  }
543:
544:  async _onClose(s, sock, code) {
545:    if (code === CONNECTION_REPLACED) {
546:      // BaÅŸka yerde aÃ§Ä±ldÄ±: otomatik yeniden baÄŸlanma YOK (AC-8).
547:      return this._terminate(
548:        s,
549:        { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'replaced', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
550:        { logout: false, deleteAuth: false },
551:      );
552:    }
553:    if (code === LOGGED_OUT) {
554:      // Telefondan Ã§Ä±karÄ±ldÄ± (AC-5): auth silinir, yeniden baÄŸlanma yok.
555:      return this._terminate(
556:        s,
557:        { status: 'logged_out', error_code: null, qr: null, qr_expires_at: null, phone_masked: null },
558:        { logout: false, deleteAuth: true },
559:      );
560:    }
561:    // GeÃ§ici kopma: Ã¼stel backoff ile yeniden aÃ§; auth korunur.
562:    const delay = backoffDelay(s.retries, this.rnd);
563:    s.retries += 1;
564:    s.sock = null;
565:    this.counters.reconnects += 1;
566:    log('baÄŸlantÄ± koptu, yeniden denenecek', { agent_id: s.agentId, status_code: code, retry_in_ms: delay });
567:    this._schedule(() => this._guard('yeniden baÄŸlanma', () => this._reopen(s)), delay);
568:  }
569:
570:  async _reopen(s) {
571:    if (this.sessions.get(s.agentId) !== s || s.sock) return;
572:    await this._startOrFail(s);
573:  }
574:
575:  async _onCreds(s, sock, saveCreds) {
576:    if (!this._isCurrent(s, sock)) return;
577:    try {
578:      await saveCreds();
579:    } catch (e) {
580:      // KayÄ±t baÅŸarÄ±sÄ±z: baÄŸlantÄ± kalÄ±cÄ± deÄŸil; kapat, temizle (AC-S3).
581:      await this._terminate(
582:        s,
583:        { status: 'error', error_code: 'creds_persist_failed', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
584:        { logout: true, deleteAuth: true },
585:      );
586:    }
587:  }
588:
589:  // EÅŸleÅŸtirme oturumu 45 sn iÃ§inde QR Ã¼retmediyse kapat (AC-7). AyrÄ± zamanlayÄ±cÄ± yok:
590:  // start() dÃ¶ngÃ¼sÃ¼ her saniye tarar (oturum baÅŸÄ±na timer kurulmaz).
591:  async _sweepNoQr() {
592:    const nowMs = this.now();
593:    for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {
594:      if (!s.fresh || s.opened || s.qrCount > 0 || !s.sock) continue;
595:      if (nowMs - s.startedAt < NO_QR_TIMEOUT_MS) continue;
596:      await this._guard('QR zaman aÅŸÄ±mÄ±', () => this._terminate(
597:        s,
598:        { status: 'error', error_code: 'no_qr_generated', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
599:        { logout: true, deleteAuth: true },
600:      ));
601:    }
602:  }
603:
604:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
605:  // Abonelik (AC-9)
606:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
607:
608:  async _checkSubscriptions() {
609:    for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {
610:      let active;
611:      try {
612:        active = await this.rest.isSubscriptionActive(s.agentId);
613:      } catch (e) {
614:        // GeÃ§ici DB hatasÄ± oturumu kapatmaz; bir sonraki turda tekrar bakÄ±lÄ±r.
615:        continue;
616:      }
617:      if (!active && this.sessions.get(s.agentId) === s) {
618:        await this._terminate(
619:          s,
620:          { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'subscription_inactive', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
621:          { logout: true, deleteAuth: true },
622:        );
623:      }
624:    }
625:  }
626:
627:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
628:  // Ortak sonlandÄ±rma: oturumu kaldÄ±r, istenirse logout + auth sil, durum + olay yaz.
629:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
630:
631:  async _terminate(s, fields, { logout = false, deleteAuth = false } = {}) {
632:    // Ä°dempotent: aynÄ± oturum iÃ§in ikinci sonlandÄ±rma (Ã¶r. iki sÃ¼pÃ¼rmenin aynÄ± anda
633:    // yakaladÄ±ÄŸÄ± oturum) durum yazmaz ve olay Ã¼retmez.
634:    if (s.terminated) return;
635:    s.terminated = true;
636:    if (this.sessions.get(s.agentId) === s) this.sessions.delete(s.agentId);
637:    const sock = s.sock;
638:    s.sock = null;
639:    if (logout && sock) closeSocket(sock);
640:    if (deleteAuth) removeAgentAuth(this.authRoot, s.agentId);
641:
642:    this.counters.terminated += 1;
643:    const from = s.status;
644:    s.status = fields.status;
645:    log('oturum sonlandÄ±', { agent_id: s.agentId, to_status: fields.status, error_code: fields.error_code || null });
646:
647:    await this.rest.update(s.agentId, {
648:      ...fields,
649:      requested_action: null,
650:      last_seen_at: this._iso(this.now()),
651:    });
652:    await this.rest.insertEvent(s.agentId, {
653:      attempt_id: s.attemptId,
654:      from_status: from,
655:      to_status: fields.status,
656:      error_code: fields.error_code || null,
657:    });
658:  }

56:function toGroupRows(agentId, metadataList) {
57:  if (!Array.isArray(metadataList)) return [];
58:  const rows = [];
59:  for (const g of metadataList) {
60:    const jid = g && g.id;
61:    if (typeof jid !== 'string' || jid.length === 0 || jid.length > MAX_JID_LENGTH) continue;
62:    const name = typeof g.subject === 'string' && g.subject.length > 0 ? g.subject : null;
63:    rows.push({ agent_id: agentId, group_jid: jid, name });
64:  }
65:  return rows;
66:}

80:    unwrapped.videoMessage?.caption ||

FILE:scraper/sidecar/sessions/supabase-rest.js
1:// EML-105 / AC-S6: Sidecar'Ä±n Supabase (PostgREST) istemcisi. Yeni baÄŸÄ±mlÄ±lÄ±k yok: global fetch.
2:// Yazma kapsamÄ±: wa_connections (yalnÄ±z ALLOWED_UPDATE_FIELDS), wa_connection_events,
3:// wa_connections silme, sidecar_heartbeat. BaÅŸka tabloya yazmaz.
4:// Her agent sorgusu agent_id eq filtreli; istek alma sorgusu yalnÄ±z requested_action dolu satÄ±rlar.
5:const { isValidAgentId } = require('./paths');
6:
7:const ALLOWED_UPDATE_FIELDS = new Set([
8:  'status',
9:  'qr',
10:  'qr_expires_at',
11:  'qr_count',
12:  'phone_masked',
13:  'error_code',
14:  'last_seen_at',
15:  'requested_action',
16:  'updated_at',
17:]);
18:
19:const ACTIVE_STATUSES = new Set(['active', 'trialing', 'past_due']);
20:
21:class SupabaseRest {
22:  constructor({ url, serviceKey, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, now = Date.now }) {
23:    if (!url || !serviceKey) throw new Error('SUPABASE_URL ve SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY gerekli');
24:    this.base = `${String(url).replace(/\/+$/, '')}/rest/v1`;
25:    this.serviceKey = serviceKey;
26:    this.fetch = fetchImpl;
27:    this.now = now;
28:  }
29:
30:  _headers(extra = {}) {
31:    return {
32:      apikey: this.serviceKey,
33:      Authorization: `Bearer ${this.serviceKey}`,
34:      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
35:      ...extra,
36:    };
37:  }
38:
39:  async _request(method, pathAndQuery, { body, prefer } = {}) {
40:    const res = await this.fetch(`${this.base}/${pathAndQuery}`, {
41:      method,
42:      headers: this._headers(prefer ? { Prefer: prefer } : {}),
43:      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
44:    });
45:    if (!res.ok) {
46:      // Hata metninde anahtar/gÃ¶vde yok; yalnÄ±z yÃ¶ntem, tablo yolu ve HTTP kodu.
47:      throw new Error(`supabase ${method} ${pathAndQuery.split('?')[0]} -> ${res.status}`);
48:    }
49:    if (res.status === 204) return null;
50:    const text = await res.text();
51:    return text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
52:  }
53:
54:  _agentFilter(agentId) {
55:    if (!isValidAgentId(agentId)) throw new Error('geÃ§ersiz agent_id');
56:    return `agent_id=eq.${agentId}`;
57:  }
58:
59:  // Bekleyen connect/disconnect istekleri (poll).
60:  async fetchPendingRequests() {
61:    const rows = await this._request(
62:      'GET',
63:      'wa_connections?select=agent_id,requested_action,attempt_id&requested_action=not.is.null',
64:    );
65:    return Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
66:  }
67:
68:  // YalnÄ±z izinli durum alanlarÄ± yazÄ±lÄ±r; diÄŸer alan denenirse hata.
69:  async update(agentId, fields) {
70:    const filter = this._agentFilter(agentId);
71:    const keys = Object.keys(fields || {});
72:    const disallowed = keys.filter((k) => !ALLOWED_UPDATE_FIELDS.has(k));
73:    if (disallowed.length > 0) throw new Error(`izinsiz alan: ${disallowed.join(',')}`);
74:    if (keys.length === 0) return;
75:    await this._request('PATCH', `wa_connections?${filter}`, {
76:      body: fields,
77:      prefer: 'return=minimal',
78:    });
79:  }
80:
81:  async insertEvent(agentId, ev) {
82:    if (!isValidAgentId(agentId)) throw new Error('geÃ§ersiz agent_id');
83:    await this._request('POST', 'wa_connection_events', {
84:      body: {
85:        agent_id: agentId,
86:        attempt_id: ev.attempt_id ?? null,
87:        from_status: ev.from_status ?? null,
88:        to_status: ev.to_status ?? null,
89:        error_code: ev.error_code ?? null,
90:      },
91:      prefer: 'return=minimal',
92:    });
93:  }
94:
95:  async deleteConnection(agentId) {
96:    const filter = this._agentFilter(agentId);
97:    await this._request('DELETE', `wa_connections?${filter}`, { prefer: 'return=minimal' });
98:  }
99:
100:  // Tek satÄ±r (id=1) upsert: yalnÄ±z zaman, oturum sayÄ±sÄ±, bayrak. counters BU upsert'te gÃ¶nderilmez
101:  // (PostgREST upsert counters kolonunu komple ezer ve 'orchestrator' alt nesnesini silerdi).
102:  // SayaÃ§lar ayrÄ± RPC ile yalnÄ±z 'sidecar' alt anahtarÄ±na yazÄ±lÄ±r (DB: counters || {sidecar: ...}).
103:  async heartbeat({ sessions_open, misconfigured, counters }) {
104:    await this._request('POST', 'sidecar_heartbeat?on_conflict=id', {
105:      body: {
106:        id: 1,
107:        last_beat_at: new Date(this.now()).toISOString(),
108:        sessions_open: Number.isInteger(sessions_open) ? sessions_open : 0,
109:        misconfigured: misconfigured === true,
110:      },
111:      prefer: 'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal',
112:    });
113:    await this._request('POST', 'rpc/set_sidecar_counters', {
114:      body: { p_counters: counters && typeof counters === 'object' ? counters : {} },
115:      prefer: 'return=minimal',
116:    });
117:  }
118:
119:  // Abonelik eriÅŸimi: active/trialing/past_due ve dÃ¶nem sonu gelmemiÅŸ (AC-9).
120:  async isSubscriptionActive(agentId) {
121:    const filter = this._agentFilter(agentId);
122:    const rows = await this._request('GET', `subscriptions?select=status,current_period_end&${filter}`);
123:    const nowMs = this.now();
124:    return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).some(
125:      (r) => ACTIVE_STATUSES.has(r.status) && Date.parse(r.current_period_end) > nowMs,
126:    );
127:  }
128:
129:  async listConnectedAgents() {
130:    const rows = await this._request('GET', 'wa_connections?select=agent_id&status=eq.connected');
131:    return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).map((r) => r.agent_id).filter(isValidAgentId);
132:  }
133:}
134:
135:module.exports = { SupabaseRest, ALLOWED_UPDATE_FIELDS };

50:def _personal_outcome_key(status: str, score: Optional[float]) -> str:
51:    """KiÅŸisel yazÄ±m sonucunu sayaÃ§ anahtarÄ±na Ã§evirir (DavranÄ±ÅŸ SÃ¶zleÅŸmesi satÄ±r 1-3, 7, seÃ§im).
52:
53:    Bu anahtarlarÄ± DB (ingest_personal_listing) zaten sayar; burada yalnÄ±z iÃ§eriksiz log iÃ§in kullanÄ±lÄ±r.
54:    """
55:    if status == "approved":
56:        return "personal_auto_approved"
57:    if status == "duplicate":
58:        return "personal_duplicate"
59:    if status == "unselected":
60:        return "personal_unselected"
61:    if score is None:
62:        return "personal_queued_noscore"
63:    return "personal_queued"

78:def _handle_personal(
79:    client,
80:    agent_id: str,
81:    payload: dict,
82:    message_id: str,
83:    text: str,
84:    parsed: dict,

87:
88:    GÃ¶nderen numarasÄ± record'a yazÄ±lmaz (AC-S4). EÅŸik: quality_score >= eÅŸik ise approved,
89:    aksi halde pending (admin kuyruÄŸu). Yazma hatasÄ± yutulur; fallback yapÄ±lmaz (kÄ±smi yazÄ±m yok).
90:    """
91:    score, _issues = score_listing(parsed)
92:    # EÅŸik kararÄ± DB'de yeniden verilir (ingest_personal_listing); buradaki deÄŸer yalnÄ±z kayÄ±t ipucudur.
93:    threshold, _fallback = get_threshold(client)
94:    status = "approved" if score is not None and score >= threshold else "pending"
95:    record = {
96:        "source": "whatsapp",
97:        "source_group": payload.get("group"),
98:        "source_group_name": payload.get("groupName"),
99:        "source_message_id": message_id or None,
100:        "raw_text": text,
101:        "parsed": parsed,
102:        "fingerprint": compute_fingerprint(parsed),
103:        "quality_score": score,
104:        "status": status,
105:    }
106:    try:
107:        outcome = ingest_personal_listing(client, agent_id, record)
108:    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - iÃ§eriksiz logla, worker'Ä± dÃ¼ÅŸÃ¼rme
109:        logger.exception("KiÅŸisel ilan yazÄ±lamadÄ± (iÃ§erik loglanmaz)")
110:        return _count_personal(client, "personal_write_error", agent_id)
111:
112:    return _log_personal_outcome(_personal_outcome_key(outcome, score), agent_id)

115:def handle_message(payload: dict) -> Optional[str]:
116:    message_id = str(payload.get("id") or "")
117:    text = (payload.get("text") or "").strip()
118:    group = payload.get("group")
119:    agent_id = payload.get("agentId")
120:
121:    if not text:
122:        return None
123:
124:    try:
125:        client = get_client()
126:        key_manager = get_default_manager()
127:
128:        if agent_id:
129:            # KVKK: seÃ§im Ã¶nbelleÄŸi (sidecar, 60 sn) eski olabilir; metin parse'a (Gemini) gitmeden
130:            # Ã¶nce grubun bu emlakÃ§Ä± iÃ§in HÃ‚LÃ‚ seÃ§ili olduÄŸu DB'den doÄŸrulanÄ±r.
131:            try:
132:                selected = is_group_selected(client, agent_id, group)
133:            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - pesimist: doÄŸrulanamazsa parse etme, iÃ§erik loglanmaz
134:                logger.warning("SeÃ§im doÄŸrulanamadÄ±, mesaj parse edilmedi (iÃ§erik loglanmaz)")
135:                return _count_personal(client, "personal_selection_check_error", agent_id)
136:            if not selected:
137:                return _count_personal(client, "personal_unselected", agent_id)
138:            try:
139:                parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
140:            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - iÃ§erik loglanmaz, yalnÄ±z sayaÃ§
141:                return _count_personal(client, "personal_parse_error", agent_id)
142:            if parsed is None:
143:                return _count_personal(client, "personal_not_listing", agent_id)
144:            return _handle_personal(client, agent_id, payload, message_id, text, parsed)
145:
146:        parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
147:
148:        if parsed is None:
149:            logger.info("Mesaj ilan deÄŸil veya parse edilemedi, atlandÄ±: %s", message_id)
150:            return None
151:
152:        fingerprint = compute_fingerprint(parsed)
153:        if fingerprint_exists(client, fingerprint):
154:            logger.info("MÃ¼kerrer ilan (fingerprint eÅŸleÅŸti), atlandÄ±: %s", message_id)
155:            return None
156:
157:        score, issues = score_listing(parsed)
158:        record = {
159:            "source": "whatsapp",
160:            "source_group": group,
161:            "source_group_name": payload.get("groupName"),
162:            "source_message_id": message_id or None,
163:            "raw_text": text,
164:            "parsed": parsed,
165:            "fingerprint": fingerprint,
166:            "quality_score": score,
167:            "status": "pending",
168:            "owner_agent_id": None,
169:        }
170:        _inserted_row, inserted = insert_raw_listing(client, record)
171:        if inserted:
172:            logger.info(
173:                "raw_listings'e yazÄ±ldÄ±: %s (skor=%.2f, auto_approve_uygun=%s, sorunlar=%s)",
174:                message_id,
175:                score,
176:                is_safe_to_auto_approve(score, float(os.getenv("QUALITY_GATE_THRESHOLD", "0.5"))),
177:                issues,
178:            )
179:        else:
180:            logger.info(
181:                "MÃ¼kerrer ilan (fingerprint Ã§akÄ±ÅŸmasÄ±, DB seviyesinde atlandÄ±): %s",
182:                message_id,
183:            )
184:    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - worker thread hatayÄ± yutmasÄ±n, logla ve devam et
185:        logger.exception("Mesaj iÅŸlenirken hata: %s", message_id)
186:    finally:
187:        with _active_ids_lock:
188:            _active_ids.discard(message_id)
189:    return None
190:

192:def _background_worker_loop(executor: ThreadPoolExecutor) -> None:
193:    while True:
194:        payload = message_queue.get()
195:        executor.submit(handle_message, payload)

198:class WebhookHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
199:    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802 - http.server API ismi
200:        if self.path != "/baileys-webhook":
201:            self.send_response(404)
202:            self.end_headers()
203:            return
204:
205:        secret = os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET", "")
206:        auth_header = self.headers.get("Authorization", "")
207:        token = auth_header[len("Bearer "):] if auth_header.startswith("Bearer ") else ""
208:        if not secret or not hmac.compare_digest(token, secret):
209:            body = json.dumps({"error": "unauthorized"}).encode("utf-8")
210:            self.send_response(401)
211:            self.send_header("WWW-Authenticate", "Bearer")
212:            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
213:            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
214:            self.end_headers()
215:            self.wfile.write(body)
216:            return
217:
218:        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
219:        body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""
220:        try:
221:            payload = json.loads(body or b"{}")
222:        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError):
223:            # Body iÃ§eriÄŸi loglanmaz (PII riski).
224:            logger.warning("GeÃ§ersiz webhook body (JSON/UTF-8 Ã§Ã¶zÃ¼lemedi), 400 dÃ¶nÃ¼ldÃ¼.")
225:            response = json.dumps({"error": "invalid_body"}).encode("utf-8")
226:            self.send_response(400)
227:            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
228:            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
229:            self.end_headers()
230:            self.wfile.write(response)
231:            return
232:
233:        if not payload.get("text"):
234:            # Metinsiz mesaj kuyruÄŸa alÄ±nmaz; 200 ile "iÅŸlendi" denir (WhatsApp tekrar gÃ¶ndermesin).
235:            response = b"{}"
236:            self.send_response(200)
237:            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
238:            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
239:            self.end_headers()
240:            self.wfile.write(response)
241:            return
242:
243:        message_id = str(payload.get("id") or "")
244:        with _active_ids_lock:
245:            if message_id and message_id in _active_ids:
246:                self.send_response(200)
247:                self.end_headers()
248:                return
249:            if message_id:
250:                _active_ids.add(message_id)
251:
252:        message_queue.put(payload)
253:
254:        self.send_response(200)
255:        self.end_headers()
256:
257:    def log_message(self, format: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: A002 - http.server imzasÄ±
258:        logger.debug("%s - %s", self.address_string(), format % args)

34:def insert_raw_listing(client: Client, record: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, Any]], bool]:

44:    DB seviyesinde engeller. Bu fonksiyon, o unique-violation'Ä± (23505)

46:    saÄŸlar; 23505 DIÅINDA bir hata (gerÃ§ek DB/baÄŸlantÄ±/yetki hatasÄ±) YUKARI

94:def get_threshold(client: Client) -> Tuple[float, bool]:

119:        logger.warning("GeÃ§ersiz eÅŸik deÄŸeri, varsayÄ±lan kullanÄ±lÄ±yor")

42:  } catch {

14:async function readThreshold(
15:  supabase: SupabaseClient,
16:): Promise<{ value: number; failed: boolean }> {
17:  try {
18:    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_get_threshold");
19:    if (error) return { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: true };
20:    const r = (data ?? null) as { ok?: unknown; value?: unknown } | null;
21:    if (r === null || r.ok !== true) return { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: true };
22:    return typeof r.value === "number"
23:      ? { value: r.value, failed: false }
24:      : { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: false };
25:  } catch {
26:    return { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: true };
27:  }

5: * Ã§aÄŸrÄ±lÄ±r (cron route). Ä°stemci girdisi almaz.
6: */
7:
8:/** EriÅŸimi olan abonelik durumlarÄ± (AC-3). */
9:export const ACCESS_STATUSES = ["active", "trialing", "past_due"] as const;
10:
11:const PAGE_SIZE = 1000;
12:
13:export type PushTarget = {
14:  id: string;
15:  agent_id: string;
16:  endpoint: string;
17:  p256dh: string;
18:  auth: string;
19:  /** Yeni ilan sayÄ±mÄ±nÄ±n baÅŸlangÄ±cÄ±: coalesce(last_notified_at, created_at). */
20:  since: string;
21:};
22:
23:type PushRow = {
24:  id: string;
25:  agent_id: string;
26:  endpoint: string;
27:  p256dh: string;
28:  auth: string;
29:  created_at: string;
30:  last_notified_at: string | null;
31:};
32:
33:type PageResult<T> = { data: T[] | null; error: unknown };
34:
35:/**
36: * PostgREST varsayÄ±lan olarak 1000 satÄ±r dÃ¶ndÃ¼rÃ¼r; tÃ¼m sayfalarÄ± sÄ±rayla toplar.
37: * `order("id")` ile sayfalar kararlÄ± tutulur.
38: */
39:async function selectAll<T>(
40:  run: (from: number, to: number) => PromiseLike<PageResult<T>>,
41:): Promise<PageResult<T>> {
42:  const out: T[] = [];
43:  for (let from = 0; ; from += PAGE_SIZE) {
44:    const { data, error } = await run(from, from + PAGE_SIZE - 1);
45:    if (error) return { data: null, error };
46:    const page = data ?? [];
47:    out.push(...page);
48:    if (page.length < PAGE_SIZE) return { data: out, error: null };
49:  }
50:}
51:
52:/**
53: * EriÅŸimi olan (subscriptions: active/trialing/past_due ve current_period_end > now)
54: * agent'larÄ±n push abonelik satÄ±rlarÄ±nÄ± dÃ¶ner. EriÅŸimi olmayan satÄ±rlar silinmez,
55: * yalnÄ±z burada atlanÄ±r (AC-3).
56: */
57:export async function loadTargets(
58:  admin: SupabaseClient,
59:): Promise<{ data: PushTarget[] | null; error: unknown }> {
60:  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
61:
62:  const subs = await selectAll<{ id: string; agent_id: string }>((from, to) =>
63:    admin
64:      .from("subscriptions")
65:      .select("id, agent_id")
66:      .in("status", [...ACCESS_STATUSES])
67:      .gt("current_period_end", nowIso)
68:      .order("id")
69:      .range(from, to),
70:  );
71:  if (subs.error) return { data: null, error: subs.error };
72:
73:  const accessible = new Set((subs.data ?? []).map((s) => s.agent_id));
74:  if (accessible.size === 0) return { data: [], error: null };
75:
76:  const rows = await selectAll<PushRow>((from, to) =>
77:    admin
78:      .from("push_subscriptions")
79:      .select("id, agent_id, endpoint, p256dh, auth, created_at, last_notified_at")
80:      .order("id")
81:      .range(from, to),
82:  );
83:  if (rows.error) return { data: null, error: rows.error };
84:
85:  const data: PushTarget[] = (rows.data ?? [])
86:    .filter((r) => accessible.has(r.agent_id))
87:    .map((r) => ({
88:      id: r.id,
89:      agent_id: r.agent_id,
90:      endpoint: r.endpoint,
91:      p256dh: r.p256dh,
92:      auth: r.auth,
93:      since: r.last_notified_at ?? r.created_at,
94:    }));
95:
96:  return { data, error: null };
97:}
98:
99:const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
100:
101:/**
102: * `since` anÄ±ndan sonra onaylanmÄ±ÅŸ ilan sayÄ±sÄ±, hedef agent'a gÃ¶rÃ¼nÃ¼r olanlar:
103: * ortak havuz (owner_agent_id is null) ve agent'Ä±n KENDÄ° kiÅŸisel ilanlarÄ± (AC-S1).
104: * BaÅŸka emlakÃ§Ä±nÄ±n kiÅŸisel ilanÄ± bu agent'a sayÄ±lmaz.
105: */
106:export async function countNewListings(
107:  admin: SupabaseClient,
108:  since: string,
109:  agentId: string,
110:): Promise<{ count: number; error: unknown }> {
111:  if (!UUID_RE.test(agentId)) return { count: 0, error: new Error("invalid agent_id") };
112:  const { count, error } = await admin
113:    .from("listings")
114:    .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })
115:    .gt("created_at", since)
116:    .or(`owner_agent_id.is.null,owner_agent_id.eq.${agentId}`);
117:  if (error) return { count: 0, error };
118:  return { count: count ?? 0, error: null };
119:}
120:
121:/** BaÅŸarÄ±lÄ± gÃ¶nderimden sonra yalnÄ±z o satÄ±rÄ±n last_notified_at deÄŸerini yazar. */
122:export async function markNotified(
123:  admin: SupabaseClient,
124:  subscriptionId: string,
125:  notifiedAt: string,
126:): Promise<{ error: unknown }> {
127:  const { error } = await admin
128:    .from("push_subscriptions")
129:    .update({ last_notified_at: notifiedAt })
130:    .eq("id", subscriptionId);
131:  return { error: error ?? null };
132:}
133:
134:/** 404/410 (push servisi aboneliÄŸi geÃ§ersiz saydÄ±) â†’ satÄ±rÄ± id ile siler. */
135:export async function removeSubscription(
136:  admin: SupabaseClient,
137:  subscriptionId: string,
138:): Promise<{ error: unknown }> {
139:  const { error } = await admin
140:    .from("push_subscriptions")
141:    .delete()
142:    .eq("id", subscriptionId);
143:  return { error: error ?? null };
144:}

672:create or replace function ingest_personal_listing(p_agent_id uuid, p_record jsonb)
673:returns text
674:language plpgsql
675:security definer
676:set search_path = public
677:as $$
678:declare
679:    v_value jsonb;
680:    v_threshold numeric := 0.70;
681:    v_fallback boolean := false;
682:    v_score numeric;
683:    v_approve boolean;
684:    v_parsed jsonb;
685:    v_raw_id uuid;
686:begin
687:    if p_agent_id is null or p_record is null or jsonb_typeof(p_record) <> 'object' then
688:        raise exception 'invalid_record';
689:    end if;
690:
691:    -- SeÃ§im kontrolÃ¼ (yazmadan Ã¶nce). source_group null ise eÅŸleÅŸme olmaz -> unselected.
692:    if not exists (
693:        select 1 from wa_group_selection ws
694:        where ws.agent_id = p_agent_id
695:          and ws.group_jid = p_record->>'source_group'
696:    ) then
697:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_unselected');
698:        return 'unselected';
699:    end if;
700:
701:    -- EÅŸik: satÄ±r yoksa / okunamazsa / geÃ§ersizse varsayÄ±lan 0.70 (threshold_fallback).
702:    select s.value into v_value
703:    from app_settings s
704:    where s.key = 'personal_auto_approve_threshold';
705:
706:    if not found then
707:        v_fallback := true;
708:    else
709:        begin
710:            v_threshold := (v_value #>> '{}')::numeric;
711:        exception when others then
712:            v_threshold := null;
713:        end;
714:    end if;
715:
716:    if v_threshold is null or v_threshold < 0 or v_threshold > 1 then
717:        v_threshold := 0.70;
718:        v_fallback := true;
719:    end if;
720:
721:    if v_fallback then
722:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('threshold_fallback');
723:    end if;
724:
725:    -- Skor: null / sayÄ± deÄŸil / 0-1 dÄ±ÅŸÄ± -> pending (kuyruk).
726:    v_score := case
727:        when p_record->>'quality_score' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (p_record->>'quality_score')::numeric
728:    end;
729:    if v_score is not null and (v_score < 0 or v_score > 1) then
730:        v_score := null;
731:    end if;
732:
733:    v_approve := v_score is not null and v_score >= v_threshold;
734:    v_parsed := p_record->'parsed';
735:
736:    begin
737:        insert into raw_listings (
738:            source, source_group, source_message_id, raw_text, parsed, fingerprint,
739:            quality_score, status, owner_agent_id, source_group_name, reviewed_at
740:        )
741:        values (
742:            'whatsapp_personal',
743:            p_record->>'source_group',
744:            case when p_record->>'source_message_id' is not null
745:                 then p_agent_id::text || ':' || (p_record->>'source_message_id')
746:            end,
747:            p_record->>'raw_text',
748:            v_parsed,
749:            p_record->>'fingerprint',
750:            v_score,
751:            case when v_approve then 'approved' else 'pending' end,
752:            p_agent_id,
753:            p_record->>'source_group_name',
754:            case when v_approve then now() end
755:        )
756:        returning id into v_raw_id;
757:    exception when unique_violation then
758:        -- AynÄ± emlakÃ§Ä±da aynÄ± fingerprint/mesaj: tek kayÄ±t kalÄ±r, yazma yok (AC-11).
759:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_duplicate');
760:        return 'duplicate';
761:    end;
762:
763:    if v_approve then
764:        insert into listings (
765:            raw_listing_id, title, price, currency, rooms, area_m2, city, district,
766:            neighborhood, description, source_group_name, owner_agent_id
767:        )
768:        values (
769:            v_raw_id,
770:            coalesce(v_parsed->>'title', trim(concat(v_parsed->>'rooms', ' ', v_parsed->>'district', ' ', v_parsed->>'city'))),
771:            (v_parsed->>'price')::numeric,
772:            coalesce(v_parsed->>'currency', 'TRY'),
773:            v_parsed->>'rooms',
774:            (v_parsed->>'area_m2')::numeric,
775:            v_parsed->>'city',
776:            v_parsed->>'district',
777:            v_parsed->>'neighborhood',
778:            v_parsed->>'description',
779:            p_record->>'source_group_name',
780:            p_agent_id
781:        );

783:        return 'approved';
784:    end if;
785:
786:    perform bump_sidecar_counter(
787:        case when v_score is null then 'personal_queued_noscore' else 'personal_queued' end
788:    );
789:    return 'pending';
790:end;

50:def _personal_outcome_key(status: str, score: Optional[float]) -> str:
51:    """KiÅŸisel yazÄ±m sonucunu sayaÃ§ anahtarÄ±na Ã§evirir (DavranÄ±ÅŸ SÃ¶zleÅŸmesi satÄ±r 1-3, 7, seÃ§im).

58:        return "personal_duplicate"
59:    if status == "unselected":
60:        return "personal_unselected"
61:    if score is None:
62:        return "personal_queued_noscore"
63:    return "personal_queued"

115:def handle_message(payload: dict) -> Optional[str]:
116:    message_id = str(payload.get("id") or "")
117:    text = (payload.get("text") or "").strip()
118:    group = payload.get("group")
119:    agent_id = payload.get("agentId")
120:
121:    if not text:
122:        return None
123:
124:    try:
125:        client = get_client()
126:        key_manager = get_default_manager()
127:
128:        if agent_id:
129:            # KVKK: seÃ§im Ã¶nbelleÄŸi (sidecar, 60 sn) eski olabilir; metin parse'a (Gemini) gitmeden
130:            # Ã¶nce grubun bu emlakÃ§Ä± iÃ§in HÃ‚LÃ‚ seÃ§ili olduÄŸu DB'den doÄŸrulanÄ±r.
131:            try:
132:                selected = is_group_selected(client, agent_id, group)
133:            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - pesimist: doÄŸrulanamazsa parse etme, iÃ§erik loglanmaz
134:                logger.warning("SeÃ§im doÄŸrulanamadÄ±, mesaj parse edilmedi (iÃ§erik loglanmaz)")
135:                return _count_personal(client, "personal_selection_check_error", agent_id)
136:            if not selected:
137:                return _count_personal(client, "personal_unselected", agent_id)
138:            try:
139:                parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
140:            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - iÃ§erik loglanmaz, yalnÄ±z sayaÃ§
141:                return _count_personal(client, "personal_parse_error", agent_id)
142:            if parsed is None:
143:                return _count_personal(client, "personal_not_listing", agent_id)
144:            return _handle_personal(client, agent_id, payload, message_id, text, parsed)
145:
146:        parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
147:
148:        if parsed is None:
149:            logger.info("Mesaj ilan deÄŸil veya parse edilemedi, atlandÄ±: %s", message_id)
150:            return None
151:
152:        fingerprint = compute_fingerprint(parsed)
153:        if fingerprint_exists(client, fingerprint):
154:            logger.info("MÃ¼kerrer ilan (fingerprint eÅŸleÅŸti), atlandÄ±: %s", message_id)
155:            return None
156:
157:        score, issues = score_listing(parsed)
158:        record = {
159:            "source": "whatsapp",
160:            "source_group": group,
161:            "source_group_name": payload.get("groupName"),
162:            "source_message_id": message_id or None,
163:            "raw_text": text,
164:            "parsed": parsed,
165:            "fingerprint": fingerprint,
166:            "quality_score": score,
167:            "status": "pending",
168:            "owner_agent_id": None,
169:        }
170:        _inserted_row, inserted = insert_raw_listing(client, record)
171:        if inserted:
172:            logger.info(
173:                "raw_listings'e yazÄ±ldÄ±: %s (skor=%.2f, auto_approve_uygun=%s, sorunlar=%s)",
174:                message_id,
175:                score,
176:                is_safe_to_auto_approve(score, float(os.getenv("QUALITY_GATE_THRESHOLD", "0.5"))),
177:                issues,
178:            )
179:        else:
180:            logger.info(
181:                "MÃ¼kerrer ilan (fingerprint Ã§akÄ±ÅŸmasÄ±, DB seviyesinde atlandÄ±): %s",
182:                message_id,
183:            )
184:    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - worker thread hatayÄ± yutmasÄ±n, logla ve devam et
185:        logger.exception("Mesaj iÅŸlenirken hata: %s", message_id)
186:    finally:
187:        with _active_ids_lock:
188:            _active_ids.discard(message_id)
189:    return None

192:def _background_worker_loop(executor: ThreadPoolExecutor) -> None:
193:    while True:
194:        payload = message_queue.get()
195:        executor.submit(handle_message, payload)

198:class WebhookHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
199:    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802 - http.server API ismi
200:        if self.path != "/baileys-webhook":
201:            self.send_response(404)
202:            self.end_headers()
203:            return
204:
205:        secret = os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET", "")
206:        auth_header = self.headers.get("Authorization", "")
207:        token = auth_header[len("Bearer "):] if auth_header.startswith("Bearer ") else ""
208:        if not secret or not hmac.compare_digest(token, secret):
209:            body = json.dumps({"error": "unauthorized"}).encode("utf-8")
210:            self.send_response(401)
211:            self.send_header("WWW-Authenticate", "Bearer")
212:            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
213:            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
214:            self.end_headers()
215:            self.wfile.write(body)
216:            return
217:
218:        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
219:        body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""
220:        try:
221:            payload = json.loads(body or b"{}")
222:        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError):
223:            # Body iÃ§eriÄŸi loglanmaz (PII riski).
224:            logger.warning("GeÃ§ersiz webhook body (JSON/UTF-8 Ã§Ã¶zÃ¼lemedi), 400 dÃ¶nÃ¼ldÃ¼.")
225:            response = json.dumps({"error": "invalid_body"}).encode("utf-8")
226:            self.send_response(400)
227:            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
228:            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
229:            self.end_headers()
230:            self.wfile.write(response)
231:            return
232:
233:        if not payload.get("text"):
234:            # Metinsiz mesaj kuyruÄŸa alÄ±nmaz; 200 ile "iÅŸlendi" denir (WhatsApp tekrar gÃ¶ndermesin).
235:            response = b"{}"
236:            self.send_response(200)
237:            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
238:            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
239:            self.end_headers()
240:            self.wfile.write(response)
241:            return
242:
243:        message_id = str(payload.get("id") or "")
244:        with _active_ids_lock:
245:            if message_id and message_id in _active_ids:
246:                self.send_response(200)
247:                self.end_headers()
248:                return
249:            if message_id:
250:                _active_ids.add(message_id)
251:
252:        message_queue.put(payload)
253:
254:        self.send_response(200)
255:        self.end_headers()
256:
257:    def log_message(self, format: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: A002 - http.server imzasÄ±
258:        logger.debug("%s - %s", self.address_string(), format % args)
259:

261:def run() -> None:
262:    if not os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET", ""):
263:        logger.error("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET env deÄŸiÅŸkeni set edilmemiÅŸ, sunucu baÅŸlatÄ±lmÄ±yor.")
264:        sys.exit(1)
265:
266:    host = os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
267:    port = int(os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_PORT", "8090"))
268:    executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=MAX_WORKERS)
269:
270:    worker_thread = threading.Thread(target=_background_worker_loop, args=(executor,), daemon=True)
271:    worker_thread.start()
272:
273:    server = HTTPServer((host, port), WebhookHandler)
274:    logger.info("Scraper worker %s:%s/baileys-webhook adresinde dinliyor.", host, port)
275:    server.serve_forever()

28:const NO_QR_TIMEOUT_MS = 45_000;

41:function backoffDelay(retries, rnd = Math.random) {
42:  const base = Math.min(BACKOFF_MAX_MS, BACKOFF_BASE_MS * 2 ** retries);
43:  const jitter = 0.9 + rnd() * 0.2; // Â±%10
44:  return Math.min(BACKOFF_MAX_MS, Math.round(base * jitter));
45:}
