65:class SessionManager {
66:  constructor({
67:    rest,
68:    makeSocket,
69:    authRoot,
70:    key,
71:    maxSessions = DEFAULT_MAX_SESSIONS,
72:    now = Date.now,
73:    setTimeout: schedule = globalThis.setTimeout,
74:    clearTimeout: unschedule = globalThis.clearTimeout,
75:    rnd = Math.random,
76:    webhook = null,
77:  }) {
78:    this.rest = rest;
79:    this.makeSocket = makeSocket;
80:    this.authRoot = authRoot;
81:    this.key = key || null;
82:    this.maxSessions = Number.isInteger(maxSessions) && maxSessions > 0 ? maxSessions : DEFAULT_MAX_SESSIONS;
83:    this.now = now;
84:    this._schedule = schedule;
85:    this._unschedule = unschedule;
86:    this.rnd = rnd;
87:    // { url, secret, fetchImpl }: yalnÄ±z seÃ§ili grup ilanlarÄ± buraya POST edilir.
88:    this.webhook = webhook;
89:
90:    this.sessions = new Map();
91:    this.running = false;
92:    this._ticking = false;
93:    this.counters = { ticks: 0, reconnects: 0, terminated: 0 };
94:    this.msgCounters = createCounters();
95:  }
96:
97:  _iso(ms) {
98:    return new Date(ms).toISOString();
99:  }
100:
101:  async _guard(label, fn) {
102:    try {
103:      await fn();
104:    } catch (e) {
105:      logError(`${label} hata`, { error: (e && (e.code || e.name)) || 'error' });
106:    }
107:  }
108:
109:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
110:  // YaÅŸam dÃ¶ngÃ¼sÃ¼
111:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
112:
113:  async start() {
114:    if (this.running) return;
115:    this.running = true;
116:
117:    if (!this.key) {
118:      logError('WA_AUTH_KEY tanÄ±mlÄ± deÄŸil; kiÅŸisel baÄŸlantÄ±lar sidecar_misconfigured ile reddedilir');
119:    }
120:
121:    await this._guard('heartbeat', () => this._beat());
122:
123:    let ids = [];
124:    if (this.key) {
125:      try {
126:        ids = await this.rest.listConnectedAgents();
127:      } catch (e) {
128:        logError('baÄŸlÄ± agent listesi alÄ±namadÄ±', { error: e.name || 'error' });
129:      }
130:    }
131:    // AÃ§Ä±lÄ±ÅŸta baÄŸlÄ± oturumlar 2 sn arayla yeniden aÃ§Ä±lÄ±r (AC-8).
132:    ids.forEach((id, i) => {
133:      this._schedule(() => this._guard('resume', () => this._resume(id)), i * RESUME_GAP_MS);
134:    });
135:
136:    this._loop(POLL_MS, () => this.tick());
137:    this._loop(NO_QR_SWEEP_MS, () => this._sweepNoQr());
138:    this._loop(HEARTBEAT_MS, () => this._beat());
139:    this._loop(SUBSCRIPTION_CHECK_MS, () => this._checkSubscriptions());
140:    this._loop(GROUP_SYNC_MS, () => this._syncAllGroups());
141:    this._loop(SELECTION_TTL_MS, () => this._refreshAllSelections());
142:  }
143:
144:  _loop(ms, fn) {
145:    const run = async () => {
146:      if (!this.running) return;
147:      await this._guard('dÃ¶ngÃ¼', fn);
148:      if (this.running) this._schedule(run, ms);
149:    };
150:    this._schedule(run, ms);
151:  }
152:
153:  stop() {
154:    this.running = false;
155:    for (const s of this.sessions.values()) {
156:      if (s.sock) {
157:        try {
158:          if (typeof s.sock.end === 'function') s.sock.end(undefined);
159:        } catch (_) {
160:          // yoksay
161:        }
162:      }
163:    }
164:    this.sessions.clear();
165:  }
166:
167:  async _beat() {
168:    await this.rest.heartbeat({
169:      sessions_open: this.sessions.size,
170:      misconfigured: !this.key,
171:      counters: { ...this.counters, ...this.msgCounters },
172:    });
173:  }
174:
175:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
176:  // Ä°stek poll'u
177:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
178:
179:  async tick() {
180:    if (this._ticking) return;
181:    this._ticking = true;
182:    this.counters.ticks += 1;
183:    try {
184:      const rows = await this.rest.fetchPendingRequests();
185:      for (const row of rows) {
186:        await this._guard('istek', () => this._handle(row));
187:      }
188:    } finally {
189:      this._ticking = false;
190:    }
191:  }
192:
193:  async _handle(row) {
194:    const agentId = row && row.agent_id;
195:    const attemptId = (row && row.attempt_id) || null;
196:    if (!isValidAgentId(agentId)) {
197:      logError('geÃ§ersiz agent_id iÃ§eren istek atlandÄ±');
198:      return;
199:    }
200:    if (row.requested_action === 'connect') return this._connect(agentId, attemptId);
201:    if (row.requested_action === 'disconnect') return this._disconnect(agentId, attemptId);
202:    return this.rest.update(agentId, { requested_action: null });
203:  }
204:
205:  async _connect(agentId, attemptId) {
206:    if (!this.key) {
207:      return this._failRequest(agentId, attemptId, 'sidecar_misconfigured');
208:    }
209:    if (this.sessions.has(agentId)) {
210:      return this.rest.update(agentId, { requested_action: null });
211:    }
212:    if (this.sessions.size >= this.maxSessions) {
213:      return this._failRequest(agentId, attemptId, 'capacity');
214:    }
215:
216:    // Yeni eÅŸleÅŸtirme temiz baÅŸlar: eski kalÄ±ntÄ± creds ile giriÅŸ denenmez.
217:    removeAgentAuth(this.authRoot, agentId);
218:    const s = {
219:      agentId,
220:      attemptId,
221:      sock: null,
222:      status: 'pending',
223:      qrCount: 0,
224:      opened: false,
225:      retries: 0,
226:      fresh: true,
227:    };
228:    this.sessions.set(agentId, s);
229:    await this.rest.update(agentId, { requested_action: null, last_seen_at: this._iso(this.now()) });
230:    log('baÄŸlantÄ± baÅŸlatÄ±ldÄ±', { agent_id: agentId });
231:    await this._startOrFail(s);
232:  }
233:
234:  async _failRequest(agentId, attemptId, errorCode) {
235:    await this.rest.update(agentId, {
236:      status: 'error',
237:      error_code: errorCode,
238:      requested_action: null,
239:      last_seen_at: this._iso(this.now()),
240:    });
241:    await this.rest.insertEvent(agentId, {
242:      attempt_id: attemptId,
243:      from_status: null,
244:      to_status: 'error',
245:      error_code: errorCode,
246:    });
247:    logError('istek reddedildi', { agent_id: agentId, error_code: errorCode });
248:  }
249:
250:  async _disconnect(agentId, attemptId) {
251:    const s = this.sessions.get(agentId);
252:    const from = s ? s.status : null;
253:    if (s) {
254:      s.terminated = true;
255:      this.sessions.delete(agentId);
256:      const sock = s.sock;
257:      s.sock = null;
258:      if (sock) closeSocket(sock);
259:    }
260:    removeAgentAuth(this.authRoot, agentId);
261:    await this.rest.deleteConnection(agentId);
262:    await this.rest.insertEvent(agentId, {
263:      attempt_id: attemptId,
264:      from_status: from,
265:      to_status: 'disconnected',
266:      error_code: null,
267:    });
268:    log('baÄŸlantÄ± kaldÄ±rÄ±ldÄ±', { agent_id: agentId });
269:  }
270:
271:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
272:  // Soket aÃ§ma
273:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
274:
275:  async _resume(agentId) {
276:    if (!this.key || this.sessions.has(agentId)) return;
277:    if (this.sessions.size >= this.maxSessions) {
278:      logError('kapasite dolu, aÃ§Ä±lÄ±ÅŸta oturum atlandÄ±', { agent_id: agentId });
279:      return;
280:    }
281:    // Slot await'ten Ã–NCE senkron rezerve edilir: bu sÄ±rada tick()->_connect aynÄ± agent iÃ§in
282:    // ikinci soket aÃ§amaz (_connect sessions.has ile erken dÃ¶ner).
283:    const s = this._newSession(agentId, null, 'connected', false);
284:    this.sessions.set(agentId, s);
285:
286:    let active;
287:    try {
288:      active = await this.rest.isSubscriptionActive(agentId);
289:    } catch (e) {
290:      this._release(s);
291:      throw e;
292:    }
293:    // Bekleme sÄ±rasÄ±nda oturum sonlandÄ±rÄ±ldÄ±/kaldÄ±rÄ±ldÄ± ise devam etme.
294:    if (this.sessions.get(agentId) !== s) return;
295:
296:    if (!active) {
297:      return this._terminate(
298:        s,
299:        { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'subscription_inactive', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
300:        { logout: false, deleteAuth: true },
301:      );
302:    }
303:    await this._startOrFail(s);
304:  }
305:
306:  // Rezervasyonu (henÃ¼z soket aÃ§Ä±lmamÄ±ÅŸ oturumu) geri al.
307:  _release(s) {
308:    if (this.sessions.get(s.agentId) === s) this.sessions.delete(s.agentId);
309:  }
310:
311:  _newSession(agentId, attemptId, status, fresh) {
312:    return { agentId, attemptId, sock: null, status, qrCount: 0, opened: false, retries: 0, fresh };
313:  }
314:
315:  async _startOrFail(s) {
316:    try {
317:      await this._spawn(s, s.fresh);
318:    } catch (e) {
319:      logError('oturum aÃ§Ä±lamadÄ±', { agent_id: s.agentId, error: e.name || 'error' });
320:      await this._terminate(
321:        s,
322:        { status: 'error', error_code: 'auth_load_failed', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
323:        { logout: false, deleteAuth: true },
324:      );
325:    }
326:  }
327:
328:  async _spawn(s, fresh) {
329:    const { state, saveCreds } = await useEncryptedAuthState(
330:      agentAuthDir(this.authRoot, s.agentId),
331:      this.key,
332:    );
333:    // Auth okunurken oturum iptal edildiyse soket aÃ§Ä±lmaz.
334:    if (this.sessions.get(s.agentId) !== s) return;
335:
336:    // AynÄ± oturumda canlÄ± bir soket varsa yenisini aÃ§madan Ã¶nce kapat (sÄ±zÄ±ntÄ± olmasÄ±n).
337:    if (s.sock) {
338:      closeSocket(s.sock);
339:      s.sock = null;
340:    }
341:
342:    const sock = this.makeSocket({
343:      agentId: s.agentId,
344:      auth: state,
345:      syncFullHistory: false,
346:      markOnlineOnConnect: false,
347:    });
348:    s.sock = sock;
349:    s.fresh = fresh;
350:    s.startedAt = this.now();
351:
352:    sock.ev.on('connection.update', (u) => this._guard('baÄŸlantÄ± olayÄ±', () => this._onUpdate(s, sock, u)));
353:    sock.ev.on('creds.update', () => this._guard('creds olayÄ±', () => this._onCreds(s, sock, saveCreds)));
354:    sock.ev.on('groups.update', () => this._guard('grup gÃ¼ncelleme', () => this._syncGroups(s, sock)));
355:  }
356:
357:  _isCurrent(s, sock) {
358:    return this.sessions.get(s.agentId) === s && s.sock === sock;
359:  }
360:
361:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
362:  // Baileys olaylarÄ±
363:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
364:
365:  async _onUpdate(s, sock, u) {
366:    if (!this._isCurrent(s, sock)) return;
367:    const { connection, lastDisconnect, qr } = u || {};
368:    if (qr) return this._onQr(s, qr);
369:    if (connection === 'open') return this._onOpen(s, sock);
370:    if (connection === 'close') {
371:      return this._onClose(s, sock, lastDisconnect && lastDisconnect.error && lastDisconnect.error.output
372:        ? lastDisconnect.error.output.statusCode
373:        : undefined);
374:    }
375:    return undefined;
376:  }
377:
378:  async _onQr(s, qr) {
379:    s.qrCount += 1;
380:    if (s.qrCount > QR_MAX_SHOWS) {
381:      return this._terminate(
382:        s,
383:        { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'qr_expired', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
384:        { logout: true, deleteAuth: true },
385:      );
386:    }
387:    const prev = s.status;
388:    s.status = 'qr';
389:    await this.rest.update(s.agentId, {
390:      status: 'qr',
391:      qr,
392:      qr_expires_at: this._iso(this.now() + QR_TTL_MS),
393:      qr_count: s.qrCount,
394:      error_code: null,
395:    });
396:    if (prev !== 'qr') {
397:      await this.rest.insertEvent(s.agentId, {
398:        attempt_id: s.attemptId,
399:        from_status: prev,
400:        to_status: 'qr',
401:        error_code: null,
402:      });
403:    }
404:  }
405:
406:  async _onOpen(s, sock) {
407:    s.opened = true;
408:    s.fresh = false;
409:    s.retries = 0;
410:    const prev = s.status;
411:    s.status = 'connected';
412:    await this.rest.update(s.agentId, {
413:      status: 'connected',
414:      phone_masked: maskPhone(sock.user && sock.user.id),
415:      qr: null,
416:      qr_expires_at: null,
417:      error_code: null,
418:      last_seen_at: this._iso(this.now()),
419:    });
420:    await this.rest.insertEvent(s.agentId, {
421:      attempt_id: s.attemptId,
422:      from_status: prev,
423:      to_status: 'connected',
424:      error_code: null,
425:    });
426:    log('baÄŸlandÄ±', { agent_id: s.agentId });
427:    // EML-107: dinleyici baÄŸlÄ±yken HER gelen mesaj sÄ±nÄ±flandÄ±rÄ±lÄ±r (seÃ§im boÅŸ olsa bile); seÃ§ili
428:    // olmayan grup/DM yalnÄ±z sayaÃ§ artÄ±rÄ±r. SeÃ§im yokluÄŸu dinleyiciyi kapatmaz.
429:    this._attachMessages(s, sock);
430:    await this._guard('grup senkronu', () => this._syncGroups(s, sock));
431:  }
432:
433:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
434:  // Gruplar ve seÃ§im (EML-107 AC-1, AC-S7)
435:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
436:
437:  // wa_groups upsert (hesap baÅŸÄ±na en fazla MAX_GROUPS); ardÄ±ndan seÃ§im yenilenir.
438:  async _syncGroups(s, sock) {
439:    if (!this._isCurrent(s, sock) || typeof sock.groupFetchAllParticipating !== 'function') return;
440:    const meta = await sock.groupFetchAllParticipating();
441:    const capped = capGroups(Object.values(meta || {}), MAX_GROUPS);
442:    if (capped.truncated) bump(this.msgCounters, 'groups_truncated');
443:    const rows = toGroupRows(s.agentId, capped.groups);
444:    s.groupNames = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.group_jid, r.name]));
445:    if (rows.length > 0) await this.rest.upsertWaGroups(s.agentId, rows);
446:    await this._refreshSelection(s);
447:  }
448:
449:  async _syncAllGroups() {
450:    for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {
451:      if (s.opened && s.sock) await this._guard('grup periyodik', () => this._syncGroups(s, s.sock));
452:    }
453:  }
454:
455:  // SeÃ§im Ã¶nbelleÄŸi (60 sn). Okuma hatasÄ±nda boÅŸ kÃ¼me: hiÃ§bir grup iÅŸlenmez (pesimist).
456:  async _refreshSelection(s) {
457:    let set;
458:    try {
459:      const jids = await this.rest.fetchSelection(s.agentId);
460:      set = new Set(Array.isArray(jids) ? jids : []);
461:    } catch (e) {
462:      bump(this.msgCounters, 'selection_read_error');
463:      logError('grup seÃ§imi okunamadÄ±', { agent_id: s.agentId, error: (e && e.name) || 'error' });
464:      set = new Set();
465:    }
466:    s.selection = { set, at: this.now() };
467:    return set;
468:  }
469:
470:  async _refreshAllSelections() {
471:    for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {
472:      if (s.opened && s.sock) await this._guard('seÃ§im yenileme', () => this._refreshSelection(s));
473:    }
474:  }
475:
476:  async _selectionOf(s) {
477:    if (!s.selection || this.now() - s.selection.at >= SELECTION_TTL_MS) await this._refreshSelection(s);
478:    return s.selection ? s.selection.set : new Set();
479:  }
480:
481:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
482:  // Mesaj iÅŸleme (yalnÄ±z seÃ§ili gruplar; DM ve seÃ§ilmeyenler iÃ§eriksiz sayaÃ§la dÃ¼ÅŸer)
483:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
484:
485:  // Dinleyici oturum soketine en fazla bir kez baÄŸlanÄ±r (yeniden baÄŸlanmada yeni soket iÃ§in tekrar).
486:  _attachMessages(s, sock) {
487:    if (s.msgSock === sock) return;
488:    s.msgSock = sock;
489:    sock.ev.on('messages.upsert', (u) => this._guard('mesaj olayÄ±', () => this._onMessages(s, sock, u)));
490:  }
491:
492:  async _onMessages(s, sock, u) {
493:    if (!this._isCurrent(s, sock)) return;
494:    const { messages, type } = u || {};
495:    if (type !== 'notify' || !Array.isArray(messages)) return;
496:    for (const msg of messages) {
497:      await this._guard('mesaj', () => this._onMessage(s, msg));
498:    }
499:  }
500:
501:  async _onMessage(s, msg) {
502:    // Kendi gÃ¶nderdiklerimiz gelen mesaj deÄŸildir.
503:    if (!msg || !msg.key || msg.key.fromMe) return;
504:
505:    // SÄ±nÄ±flandÄ±rma metin Ã§Ä±karmadan Ã–NCE: seÃ§ili olmayan grup/DM iÃ§eriÄŸi hiÃ§ okunmaz (AC-S4).
506:    const remoteJid = msg.key.remoteJid;
507:    const selection = await this._selectionOf(s);
508:    const outcome = classifyIncoming({ remoteJid }, selection);
509:    if (outcome !== 'process') {
510:      bump(this.msgCounters, outcome);
511:      return;
512:    }
513:
514:    // SeÃ§ili grup + metinsiz (tepki, protokol) mesaj: ilan adayÄ± deÄŸildir, sayÄ±lmaz.
515:    const text = messageText(msg.message);
516:    if (!text) return;
517:
518:    const groupName = (s.groupNames && s.groupNames.get(remoteJid)) || null;
519:    const payload = { id: msg.key.id, text, group: remoteJid, groupName, agentId: s.agentId };
520:    const ok = await this._postWebhook(payload);
521:    if (!ok) bump(this.msgCounters, 'webhook_post_failed');
522:  }
523:
524:  // Ortak kÃ¶prÃ¼nÃ¼n (bridge.js) webhook desenine uyumlu: 3 deneme, bekleme yok. Ä°Ã§erik loglanmaz.
525:  async _postWebhook(payload) {
526:    const { url, secret, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch } = this.webhook || {};
527:    if (!url || !secret) return false;
528:    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= WEBHOOK_MAX_ATTEMPTS; attempt += 1) {
529:      try {
530:        const res = await fetchImpl(url, {
531:          method: 'POST',
532:          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${secret}` },
533:          body: JSON.stringify(payload),
534:        });
535:        if (res.ok) return true;
536:        logError('webhook yanÄ±tÄ± baÅŸarÄ±sÄ±z', { status: res.status });
537:      } catch (e) {
538:        logError('webhook baÄŸlantÄ± hatasÄ±', { error: (e && e.name) || 'error' });
539:      }
540:    }
541:    return false;
542:  }
543:
544:  async _onClose(s, sock, code) {
545:    if (code === CONNECTION_REPLACED) {
546:      // BaÅŸka yerde aÃ§Ä±ldÄ±: otomatik yeniden baÄŸlanma YOK (AC-8).
547:      return this._terminate(
548:        s,
549:        { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'replaced', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
550:        { logout: false, deleteAuth: false },
551:      );
552:    }
553:    if (code === LOGGED_OUT) {
554:      // Telefondan Ã§Ä±karÄ±ldÄ± (AC-5): auth silinir, yeniden baÄŸlanma yok.
555:      return this._terminate(
556:        s,
557:        { status: 'logged_out', error_code: null, qr: null, qr_expires_at: null, phone_masked: null },
558:        { logout: false, deleteAuth: true },
559:      );
560:    }
561:    // GeÃ§ici kopma: Ã¼stel backoff ile yeniden aÃ§; auth korunur.
562:    const delay = backoffDelay(s.retries, this.rnd);
563:    s.retries += 1;
564:    s.sock = null;
565:    this.counters.reconnects += 1;
566:    log('baÄŸlantÄ± koptu, yeniden denenecek', { agent_id: s.agentId, status_code: code, retry_in_ms: delay });
567:    this._schedule(() => this._guard('yeniden baÄŸlanma', () => this._reopen(s)), delay);
568:  }
569:
570:  async _reopen(s) {
571:    if (this.sessions.get(s.agentId) !== s || s.sock) return;
572:    await this._startOrFail(s);
573:  }
574:
575:  async _onCreds(s, sock, saveCreds) {
576:    if (!this._isCurrent(s, sock)) return;
577:    try {
578:      await saveCreds();
579:    } catch (e) {
580:      // KayÄ±t baÅŸarÄ±sÄ±z: baÄŸlantÄ± kalÄ±cÄ± deÄŸil; kapat, temizle (AC-S3).
581:      await this._terminate(

584:        { logout: true, deleteAuth: true },
585:      );
586:    }
587:  }

589:  // EÅŸleÅŸtirme oturumu 45 sn iÃ§inde QR Ã¼retmediyse kapat (AC-7). AyrÄ± zamanlayÄ±cÄ± yok:
590:  // start() dÃ¶ngÃ¼sÃ¼ her saniye tarar (oturum baÅŸÄ±na timer kurulmaz).
591:  async _sweepNoQr() {
592:    const nowMs = this.now();
593:    for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {
594:      if (!s.fresh || s.opened || s.qrCount > 0 || !s.sock) continue;
595:      if (nowMs - s.startedAt < NO_QR_TIMEOUT_MS) continue;
596:      await this._guard('QR zaman aÅŸÄ±mÄ±', () => this._terminate(
597:        s,
598:        { status: 'error', error_code: 'no_qr_generated', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
599:        { logout: true, deleteAuth: true },
600:      ));
601:    }
602:  }
603:
604:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
605:  // Abonelik (AC-9)
606:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
607:
608:  async _checkSubscriptions() {
609:    for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {
610:      let active;
611:      try {
612:        active = await this.rest.isSubscriptionActive(s.agentId);
613:      } catch (e) {
614:        // GeÃ§ici DB hatasÄ± oturumu kapatmaz; bir sonraki turda tekrar bakÄ±lÄ±r.
615:        continue;
616:      }
617:      if (!active && this.sessions.get(s.agentId) === s) {
618:        await this._terminate(
619:          s,
620:          { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'subscription_inactive', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
621:          { logout: true, deleteAuth: true },
622:        );
623:      }
624:    }
625:  }

627:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
628:  // Ortak sonlandÄ±rma: oturumu kaldÄ±r, istenirse logout + auth sil, durum + olay yaz.
629:  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
630:
631:  async _terminate(s, fields, { logout = false, deleteAuth = false } = {}) {
632:    // Ä°dempotent: aynÄ± oturum iÃ§in ikinci sonlandÄ±rma (Ã¶r. iki sÃ¼pÃ¼rmenin aynÄ± anda
633:    // yakaladÄ±ÄŸÄ± oturum) durum yazmaz ve olay Ã¼retmez.
634:    if (s.terminated) return;
635:    s.terminated = true;
636:    if (this.sessions.get(s.agentId) === s) this.sessions.delete(s.agentId);
637:    const sock = s.sock;
638:    s.sock = null;
639:    if (logout && sock) closeSocket(sock);
640:    if (deleteAuth) removeAgentAuth(this.authRoot, s.agentId);
641:
642:    this.counters.terminated += 1;
643:    const from = s.status;
644:    s.status = fields.status;
645:    log('oturum sonlandÄ±', { agent_id: s.agentId, to_status: fields.status, error_code: fields.error_code || null });
646:
647:    await this.rest.update(s.agentId, {
648:      ...fields,
649:      requested_action: null,
650:      last_seen_at: this._iso(this.now()),
651:    });
652:    await this.rest.insertEvent(s.agentId, {
653:      attempt_id: s.attemptId,
654:      from_status: from,
655:      to_status: fields.status,
656:      error_code: fields.error_code || null,
657:    });
658:  }

56:function toGroupRows(agentId, metadataList) {
57:  if (!Array.isArray(metadataList)) return [];
58:  const rows = [];
59:  for (const g of metadataList) {
60:    const jid = g && g.id;
61:    if (typeof jid !== 'string' || jid.length === 0 || jid.length > MAX_JID_LENGTH) continue;
62:    const name = typeof g.subject === 'string' && g.subject.length > 0 ? g.subject : null;
63:    rows.push({ agent_id: agentId, group_jid: jid, name });
64:  }
65:  return rows;
66:}

189:revoke all on function feed_access() from public, anon, authenticated;

191:create or replace function feed_listings(p_filters jsonb default '{}'::jsonb, p_page int default 1)
192:returns jsonb
193:language plpgsql
194:security definer
195:set search_path = public
196:as $$
197:declare
198:    v_has boolean;
199:    v_lim integer;
200:    v_filters jsonb := coalesce(p_filters, '{}'::jsonb);
201:    v_page integer := greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_page, 1), 500));
202:    v_il text := v_filters->>'il';
203:    v_ilce text := v_filters->>'ilce';
204:    v_grup text := v_filters->>'grup';
205:    v_oda text := v_filters->>'oda';
206:    v_scope text := v_filters->>'scope';
207:    v_fmin numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMin' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMin')::numeric end;
208:    v_fmax numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMax' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMax')::numeric end;
209:    v_has_price boolean := v_fmin is not null or v_fmax is not null;
210:    v_items jsonb;
211:    v_locked bigint;
212:    v_open_today bigint;
213:begin
214:    select a.has_access, a.lim into v_has, v_lim from feed_access() a;
215:
216:    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
217:        return jsonb_build_object(
218:            'items', '[]'::jsonb,
219:            'locked_count', 0,
220:            'limit', 0,
221:            'reason', 'no_subscription'
222:        );
223:    end if;
224:
225:    with visible as (
226:        select l.id, l.title, l.price, l.currency, l.rooms, l.area_m2,
227:               l.city, l.district, l.source_group_name, l.created_at, l.owner_agent_id
228:        from listings l
229:        where l.owner_agent_id is null or l.owner_agent_id = auth.uid()
230:    ),
231:    ranked as (
232:        -- SÄ±ra yalnÄ±z ortak ilanlar arasÄ±nda (partition owner null) -> kiÅŸisel ilan limite sayÄ±lmaz.
233:        select
234:            v.*,
235:            row_number() over (
236:                partition by (v.owner_agent_id is null), (v.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
237:                order by v.created_at desc, v.id desc
238:            ) as rn
239:        from visible v
240:    ),
241:    flagged as (
242:        select
243:            r.*,
244:            (r.owner_agent_id is not null or v_lim is null or r.rn <= v_lim) as is_open,
245:            ((r.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
246:                = (now() at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date) as is_today
247:        from ranked r
248:    ),
249:    filtered as (
250:        select f.*
251:        from flagged f
252:        where (v_il is null or f.city = v_il)
253:          and (v_ilce is null or f.district = v_ilce)
254:          and (v_grup is null or f.source_group_name = v_grup)
255:          and (not v_has_price or (f.currency = 'TRY' and f.price is not null))
256:          and (v_fmin is null or f.price >= v_fmin)
257:          and (v_fmax is null or f.price <= v_fmax)
258:          and (
259:                v_oda is null
260:             or (v_oda = '5+' and f.rooms in ('5+1', '5+2', '6+1', '6+2', '7+1', '8+1'))
261:             or (v_oda <> '5+' and f.rooms = v_oda)
262:          )
263:          and (v_scope is distinct from 'mine' or f.owner_agent_id = auth.uid())
264:    ),
265:    page as (
266:        select f.*
267:        from filtered f
268:        order by f.created_at desc, f.id desc
269:        limit 20 offset (v_page - 1) * 20
270:    )
271:    select
272:        coalesce(
273:            jsonb_agg(
274:                case
275:                    when p.is_open then jsonb_build_object(
276:                        'id', p.id,
277:                        'title', p.title,
278:                        'price', p.price,
279:                        'currency', p.currency,
280:                        'rooms', p.rooms,
281:                        'area_m2', p.area_m2,
282:                        'city', p.city,
283:                        'district', p.district,
284:                        'source_group_name', p.source_group_name,
285:                        'created_at', p.created_at,
286:                        'personal', (p.owner_agent_id is not null),
287:                        'owner_agent_id', p.owner_agent_id,
288:                        'locked', false
289:                    )
290:                    else jsonb_build_object(
291:                        'id', p.id,
292:                        'city', p.city,
293:                        'district', p.district,
294:                        'created_at', p.created_at,
295:                        'locked', true
296:                    )
297:                end
298:                order by p.created_at desc, p.id desc
299:            ),
300:            '[]'::jsonb
301:        ),
302:        (select count(*) from filtered f2 where not f2.is_open),
303:        (select count(*) from flagged g where g.is_today and g.is_open and g.owner_agent_id is null)
304:    into v_items, v_locked, v_open_today
305:    from page p;
306:
307:    return jsonb_build_object(
308:        'items', coalesce(v_items, '[]'::jsonb),
309:        'locked_count', coalesce(v_locked, 0),
310:        'open_today', coalesce(v_open_today, 0),
311:        'limit', v_lim
312:    );
313:end;
314:$$;
315:
316:revoke all on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from public;
317:revoke execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from anon;
318:grant execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) to authenticated;

320:create or replace function feed_filter_options(p_il text default null)
321:returns jsonb
322:language plpgsql
323:security definer
324:set search_path = public
325:as $$
326:declare
327:    v_has boolean;
328:begin
329:    select a.has_access into v_has from feed_access() a;

342:            ) s

350:                  and (owner_agent_id is null or owner_agent_id = auth.uid())

355:            from (
356:                select distinct source_group_name as v from listings
357:                where source_group_name is not null
358:                  and (owner_agent_id is null or owner_agent_id = auth.uid())
359:            ) s
360:        )
361:    );
362:end;
363:$$;

403:create or replace function get_my_wa_groups()
404:returns jsonb
405:language plpgsql
406:security definer
407:set search_path = public
408:as $$
409:declare
410:    v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
411:    v_status text;
412:    v_synced timestamptz;
413:    v_groups jsonb;
414:begin
415:    if v_uid is null then
416:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized');
417:    end if;
418:
419:    select c.status into v_status
420:    from wa_connections c
421:    where c.agent_id = v_uid;
422:
423:    if not found then

425:    end if;

428:    from wa_groups g
429:    where g.agent_id = v_uid;
430:
431:    select coalesce(jsonb_agg(
432:        jsonb_build_object('jid', g.group_jid, 'name', g.name, 'selected', (s.group_jid is not null))
433:        order by g.name nulls last, g.group_jid
434:    ), '[]'::jsonb)
435:    into v_groups
436:    from wa_groups g

439:    where g.agent_id = v_uid;
440:
441:    return jsonb_build_object(
442:        'ok', true,
443:        'status', v_status,
444:        'groups', v_groups,
445:        'max', 20
446:    ) || case
447:            when v_status = 'connected' then jsonb_build_object('groups_synced_at', v_synced)
448:            else jsonb_build_object('readonly', true)
449:         end;
450:end;
451:$$;

643:-- set_sidecar_counters: sidecar kendi sayaÃ§ nesnesini YALNIZ 'sidecar' alt anahtarÄ±na yazar;
644:-- 'orchestrator' alt nesnesi korunur (PostgREST upsert counters'Ä± ezmesin diye ayrÄ± RPC).
645:create or replace function set_sidecar_counters(p_counters jsonb)
646:returns void
647:language plpgsql
648:security definer
649:set search_path = public
650:as $$
651:begin
652:    if p_counters is null or jsonb_typeof(p_counters) <> 'object' then
653:        raise exception 'invalid_counters';
654:    end if;
655:
656:    insert into sidecar_heartbeat (id, counters)
657:    values (1, jsonb_build_object('sidecar', p_counters))
658:    on conflict (id) do update
659:    set counters = coalesce(sidecar_heartbeat.counters, '{}'::jsonb)
660:                   || jsonb_build_object('sidecar', p_counters);
661:end;
662:$$;

666:-- 10) Service-role RPC: kiÅŸisel ilan giriÅŸi (AC-3, AC-5, AC-6, AC-11, AC-S3, AC-S4).
667:--     owner ASLA p_record'dan okunmaz; p_agent_id sidecar oturumunun sahibidir.
668:--     SeÃ§im otoritesi DB'dir (AC-5, AC-9): source_group bu agent'Ä±n wa_group_selection
669:--     kaydÄ±nda yoksa (seÃ§im kaldÄ±rÄ±lmÄ±ÅŸ / hiÃ§ seÃ§ilmemiÅŸ) hiÃ§bir ÅŸey yazÄ±lmaz -> 'unselected'.
670:--     DÃ¶nÃ¼ÅŸ: 'approved' | 'pending' | 'duplicate' | 'unselected'. Tek transaction: hata -> tÃ¼mÃ¼ geri alÄ±nÄ±r.
671:--     SonuÃ§ ve eÅŸik fallback'i sayaÃ§lara bu fonksiyon iÃ§inde, aynÄ± transaction'da yazÄ±lÄ±r.
672:create or replace function ingest_personal_listing(p_agent_id uuid, p_record jsonb)
673:returns text
674:language plpgsql
675:security definer
676:set search_path = public
677:as $$
678:declare
679:    v_value jsonb;
680:    v_threshold numeric := 0.70;
681:    v_fallback boolean := false;
682:    v_score numeric;
683:    v_approve boolean;
684:    v_parsed jsonb;
685:    v_raw_id uuid;
686:begin
687:    if p_agent_id is null or p_record is null or jsonb_typeof(p_record) <> 'object' then
688:        raise exception 'invalid_record';
689:    end if;
690:
691:    -- SeÃ§im kontrolÃ¼ (yazmadan Ã¶nce). source_group null ise eÅŸleÅŸme olmaz -> unselected.
692:    if not exists (
693:        select 1 from wa_group_selection ws
694:        where ws.agent_id = p_agent_id
695:          and ws.group_jid = p_record->>'source_group'
696:    ) then
697:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_unselected');
698:        return 'unselected';
699:    end if;
700:
701:    -- EÅŸik: satÄ±r yoksa / okunamazsa / geÃ§ersizse varsayÄ±lan 0.70 (threshold_fallback).
702:    select s.value into v_value
703:    from app_settings s
704:    where s.key = 'personal_auto_approve_threshold';
705:
706:    if not found then
707:        v_fallback := true;
708:    else
709:        begin
710:            v_threshold := (v_value #>> '{}')::numeric;
711:        exception when others then
712:            v_threshold := null;
713:        end;
714:    end if;
715:
716:    if v_threshold is null or v_threshold < 0 or v_threshold > 1 then
717:        v_threshold := 0.70;
718:        v_fallback := true;
719:    end if;
720:
721:    if v_fallback then
722:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('threshold_fallback');
723:    end if;
724:
725:    -- Skor: null / sayÄ± deÄŸil / 0-1 dÄ±ÅŸÄ± -> pending (kuyruk).
726:    v_score := case
727:        when p_record->>'quality_score' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (p_record->>'quality_score')::numeric
728:    end;
729:    if v_score is not null and (v_score < 0 or v_score > 1) then
730:        v_score := null;
731:    end if;
732:
733:    v_approve := v_score is not null and v_score >= v_threshold;
734:    v_parsed := p_record->'parsed';
735:
736:    begin
737:        insert into raw_listings (
738:            source, source_group, source_message_id, raw_text, parsed, fingerprint,
739:            quality_score, status, owner_agent_id, source_group_name, reviewed_at
740:        )
741:        values (
742:            'whatsapp_personal',
743:            p_record->>'source_group',
744:            case when p_record->>'source_message_id' is not null
745:                 then p_agent_id::text || ':' || (p_record->>'source_message_id')
746:            end,
747:            p_record->>'raw_text',
748:            v_parsed,
749:            p_record->>'fingerprint',
750:            v_score,
751:            case when v_approve then 'approved' else 'pending' end,
752:            p_agent_id,
753:            p_record->>'source_group_name',
754:            case when v_approve then now() end
755:        )
756:        returning id into v_raw_id;
757:    exception when unique_violation then
758:        -- AynÄ± emlakÃ§Ä±da aynÄ± fingerprint/mesaj: tek kayÄ±t kalÄ±r, yazma yok (AC-11).
759:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_duplicate');
760:        return 'duplicate';
761:    end;
762:
763:    if v_approve then
764:        insert into listings (
765:            raw_listing_id, title, price, currency, rooms, area_m2, city, district,
766:            neighborhood, description, source_group_name, owner_agent_id
767:        )
768:        values (
769:            v_raw_id,
770:            coalesce(v_parsed->>'title', trim(concat(v_parsed->>'rooms', ' ', v_parsed->>'district', ' ', v_parsed->>'city'))),
771:            (v_parsed->>'price')::numeric,
772:            coalesce(v_parsed->>'currency', 'TRY'),
773:            v_parsed->>'rooms',
774:            (v_parsed->>'area_m2')::numeric,
775:            v_parsed->>'city',
776:            v_parsed->>'district',
777:            v_parsed->>'neighborhood',
778:            v_parsed->>'description',
779:            p_record->>'source_group_name',
780:            p_agent_id
781:        );
782:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_auto_approved');
783:        return 'approved';
784:    end if;
785:
786:    perform bump_sidecar_counter(
787:        case when v_score is null then 'personal_queued_noscore' else 'personal_queued' end
788:    );
789:    return 'pending';
790:end;
791:$$;
792:
793:revoke all on function ingest_personal_listing(uuid, jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated;

30:create or replace function approve_raw_listing(p_raw_listing_id uuid)
31:returns uuid
32:language plpgsql
33:security invoker
34:set search_path = public
35:as $$
36:declare
37:    v_parsed jsonb;
38:    v_source_group_name text;
39:    v_listing_id uuid;
40:begin
41:    select parsed, source_group_name into v_parsed, v_source_group_name
42:    from raw_listings
43:    where id = p_raw_listing_id and status = 'pending';
44:
45:    if v_parsed is null then
46:        raise exception 'raw_listing bulunamadÄ± veya zaten incelenmiÅŸ: %', p_raw_listing_id;
47:    end if;
48:
49:    insert into listings (raw_listing_id, title, price, currency, rooms, area_m2, city, district, neighborhood, description, source_group_name)
50:    values (
51:        p_raw_listing_id,
52:        coalesce(v_parsed->>'title', trim(concat(v_parsed->>'rooms', ' ', v_parsed->>'district', ' ', v_parsed->>'city'))),
53:        (v_parsed->>'price')::numeric,
54:        coalesce(v_parsed->>'currency', 'TRY'),
55:        v_parsed->>'rooms',
56:        (v_parsed->>'area_m2')::numeric,
57:        v_parsed->>'city',
58:        v_parsed->>'district',
59:        v_parsed->>'neighborhood',
60:        v_parsed->>'description',
61:        v_source_group_name
62:    )
63:    returning id into v_listing_id;
64:
65:    update raw_listings
66:    set status = 'approved', reviewed_by = auth.uid(), reviewed_at = now()
67:    where id = p_raw_listing_id;
68:
69:    return v_listing_id;
70:end;
71:$$;

73:-- 4) Feed RPC'leri: 0020 hali (owner filtresi yok, scope yok).
74:create or replace function feed_listings(p_filters jsonb default '{}'::jsonb, p_page int default 1)
75:returns jsonb
76:language plpgsql
77:security definer
78:set search_path = public
79:as $$
80:declare
81:    v_has boolean;
82:    v_lim integer;
83:    v_filters jsonb := coalesce(p_filters, '{}'::jsonb);
84:    v_page integer := greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_page, 1), 500));
85:    v_il text := v_filters->>'il';
86:    v_ilce text := v_filters->>'ilce';
87:    v_grup text := v_filters->>'grup';
88:    v_oda text := v_filters->>'oda';
89:    v_fmin numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMin' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMin')::numeric end;
90:    v_fmax numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMax' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMax')::numeric end;
91:    v_has_price boolean := v_fmin is not null or v_fmax is not null;
92:    v_items jsonb;
93:    v_locked bigint;
94:    v_open_today bigint;
95:begin
96:    select a.has_access, a.lim into v_has, v_lim from feed_access() a;
97:
98:    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
99:        return jsonb_build_object(
100:            'items', '[]'::jsonb,
101:            'locked_count', 0,
102:            'limit', 0,
103:            'reason', 'no_subscription'
104:        );
105:    end if;
106:
107:    with ranked as (
108:        select
109:            l.id, l.title, l.price, l.currency, l.rooms, l.area_m2,
110:            l.city, l.district, l.source_group_name, l.created_at,
111:            row_number() over (
112:                partition by (l.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
113:                order by l.created_at desc, l.id desc
114:            ) as rn
115:        from listings l
116:    ),
117:    flagged as (
118:        select
119:            r.*,
120:            (v_lim is null or r.rn <= v_lim) as is_open,
121:            ((r.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
122:                = (now() at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date) as is_today
123:        from ranked r
124:    ),
125:    filtered as (
126:        select f.*
127:        from flagged f
128:        where (v_il is null or f.city = v_il)
129:          and (v_ilce is null or f.district = v_ilce)
130:          and (v_grup is null or f.source_group_name = v_grup)
131:          and (not v_has_price or (f.currency = 'TRY' and f.price is not null))
132:          and (v_fmin is null or f.price >= v_fmin)
133:          and (v_fmax is null or f.price <= v_fmax)
134:          and (
135:                v_oda is null
136:             or (v_oda = '5+' and f.rooms in ('5+1', '5+2', '6+1', '6+2', '7+1', '8+1'))
137:             or (v_oda <> '5+' and f.rooms = v_oda)
138:          )
139:    ),
140:    page as (
141:        select f.*
142:        from filtered f
143:        order by f.created_at desc, f.id desc
144:        limit 20 offset (v_page - 1) * 20
145:    )
146:    select
147:        coalesce(
148:            jsonb_agg(
149:                case
150:                    when p.is_open then jsonb_build_object(
151:                        'id', p.id,
152:                        'title', p.title,
153:                        'price', p.price,
154:                        'currency', p.currency,
155:                        'rooms', p.rooms,
156:                        'area_m2', p.area_m2,
157:                        'city', p.city,
158:                        'district', p.district,
159:                        'source_group_name', p.source_group_name,
160:                        'created_at', p.created_at,
161:                        'locked', false
162:                    )
163:                    else jsonb_build_object(
164:                        'id', p.id,
165:                        'city', p.city,
166:                        'district', p.district,
167:                        'created_at', p.created_at,
168:                        'locked', true
169:                    )
170:                end
171:                order by p.created_at desc, p.id desc
172:            ),
173:            '[]'::jsonb
174:        ),
175:        (select count(*) from filtered f2 where not f2.is_open),
176:        (select count(*) from flagged g where g.is_today and g.is_open)
177:    into v_items, v_locked, v_open_today
178:    from page p;
179:
180:    return jsonb_build_object(
181:        'items', coalesce(v_items, '[]'::jsonb),
182:        'locked_count', coalesce(v_locked, 0),
183:        'open_today', coalesce(v_open_today, 0),
184:        'limit', v_lim
185:    );
186:end;
187:$$;
188:
189:revoke all on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from public;
190:revoke execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from anon;
191:grant execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) to authenticated;

193:create or replace function feed_filter_options(p_il text default null)
194:returns jsonb
195:language plpgsql
196:security definer
197:set search_path = public
198:as $$
199:declare
200:    v_has boolean;
201:begin
202:    select a.has_access into v_has from feed_access() a;
203:
204:    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
205:        return jsonb_build_object('cities', '[]'::jsonb, 'districts', '[]'::jsonb, 'groups', '[]'::jsonb);
206:    end if;
207:
208:    return jsonb_build_object(
209:        'cities', (
210:            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
211:            from (select distinct city as v from listings where city is not null) s
212:        ),
213:        'districts', (
214:            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
215:            from (
216:                select distinct district as v from listings
217:                where district is not null
218:                  and (p_il is null or city = p_il)
219:            ) s
220:        ),
221:        'groups', (
222:            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
223:            from (select distinct source_group_name as v from listings where source_group_name is not null) s
224:        )
225:    );
226:end;
227:$$;
228:

231:grant execute on function feed_filter_options(text) to authenticated;

139:-- 6) AkÄ±ÅŸ RPC'leri: gÃ¶rÃ¼nÃ¼rlÃ¼k = ortak (owner null) veya kendi (owner = auth.uid()).

143:create or replace function feed_access()
144:returns table (has_access boolean, lim integer)
145:language plpgsql
146:security definer
147:set search_path = public
148:as $$
149:declare
150:    v_plan_id uuid;
151:    v_max integer;
152:begin
153:    select p.id, p.max_listings
154:      into v_plan_id, v_max
155:    from subscriptions s
156:    left join plans p on p.id = s.plan_id
157:    where s.agent_id = auth.uid()
158:      and s.status in ('active', 'trialing', 'past_due')
159:      and s.current_period_end > now()
160:    order by s.created_at desc
161:    limit 1;
162:
163:    if not found then
164:        return query select false, null::integer;
165:        return;
166:    end if;
167:
168:    if v_plan_id is null then
169:        raise log 'feed_plan_missing';
170:        return query select true, 10;
171:        return;
172:    end if;
173:
174:    if v_max is null then
175:        return query select true, null::integer;
176:        return;
177:    end if;
178:
179:    if v_max <= 0 then
180:        raise log 'feed_limit_clamped';
181:        return query select true, 0;
182:        return;
183:    end if;
184:
185:    return query select true, v_max;
186:end;
187:$$;

191:create or replace function feed_listings(p_filters jsonb default '{}'::jsonb, p_page int default 1)
192:returns jsonb
193:language plpgsql
194:security definer
195:set search_path = public
196:as $$
197:declare
198:    v_has boolean;
199:    v_lim integer;
200:    v_filters jsonb := coalesce(p_filters, '{}'::jsonb);
201:    v_page integer := greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_page, 1), 500));
202:    v_il text := v_filters->>'il';
203:    v_ilce text := v_filters->>'ilce';
204:    v_grup text := v_filters->>'grup';
205:    v_oda text := v_filters->>'oda';
206:    v_scope text := v_filters->>'scope';
207:    v_fmin numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMin' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMin')::numeric end;
208:    v_fmax numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMax' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMax')::numeric end;
209:    v_has_price boolean := v_fmin is not null or v_fmax is not null;
210:    v_items jsonb;
211:    v_locked bigint;
212:    v_open_today bigint;
213:begin
214:    select a.has_access, a.lim into v_has, v_lim from feed_access() a;
215:
216:    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
217:        return jsonb_build_object(
218:            'items', '[]'::jsonb,
219:            'locked_count', 0,
220:            'limit', 0,
221:            'reason', 'no_subscription'
222:        );
223:    end if;
224:
225:    with visible as (
226:        select l.id, l.title, l.price, l.currency, l.rooms, l.area_m2,
227:               l.city, l.district, l.source_group_name, l.created_at, l.owner_agent_id
228:        from listings l
229:        where l.owner_agent_id is null or l.owner_agent_id = auth.uid()
230:    ),
231:    ranked as (
232:        -- SÄ±ra yalnÄ±z ortak ilanlar arasÄ±nda (partition owner null) -> kiÅŸisel ilan limite sayÄ±lmaz.
233:        select
234:            v.*,
235:            row_number() over (
236:                partition by (v.owner_agent_id is null), (v.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
237:                order by v.created_at desc, v.id desc
238:            ) as rn
239:        from visible v
240:    ),
241:    flagged as (
242:        select
243:            r.*,
244:            (r.owner_agent_id is not null or v_lim is null or r.rn <= v_lim) as is_open,
245:            ((r.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
246:                = (now() at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date) as is_today
247:        from ranked r
248:    ),
249:    filtered as (
250:        select f.*
251:        from flagged f
252:        where (v_il is null or f.city = v_il)
253:          and (v_ilce is null or f.district = v_ilce)
254:          and (v_grup is null or f.source_group_name = v_grup)
255:          and (not v_has_price or (f.currency = 'TRY' and f.price is not null))
256:          and (v_fmin is null or f.price >= v_fmin)
257:          and (v_fmax is null or f.price <= v_fmax)
258:          and (
259:                v_oda is null
260:             or (v_oda = '5+' and f.rooms in ('5+1', '5+2', '6+1', '6+2', '7+1', '8+1'))
261:             or (v_oda <> '5+' and f.rooms = v_oda)
262:          )
263:          and (v_scope is distinct from 'mine' or f.owner_agent_id = auth.uid())
264:    ),
265:    page as (
266:        select f.*
267:        from filtered f
268:        order by f.created_at desc, f.id desc
269:        limit 20 offset (v_page - 1) * 20
270:    )
271:    select
272:        coalesce(
273:            jsonb_agg(
274:                case
275:                    when p.is_open then jsonb_build_object(
276:                        'id', p.id,
277:                        'title', p.title,
278:                        'price', p.price,
279:                        'currency', p.currency,
280:                        'rooms', p.rooms,
281:                        'area_m2', p.area_m2,
282:                        'city', p.city,
283:                        'district', p.district,
284:                        'source_group_name', p.source_group_name,
285:                        'created_at', p.created_at,
286:                        'personal', (p.owner_agent_id is not null),
287:                        'owner_agent_id', p.owner_agent_id,
288:                        'locked', false
289:                    )
290:                    else jsonb_build_object(
291:                        'id', p.id,
292:                        'city', p.city,
293:                        'district', p.district,
294:                        'created_at', p.created_at,
295:                        'locked', true
296:                    )
297:                end
298:                order by p.created_at desc, p.id desc
299:            ),
300:            '[]'::jsonb
301:        ),
302:        (select count(*) from filtered f2 where not f2.is_open),
303:        (select count(*) from flagged g where g.is_today and g.is_open and g.owner_agent_id is null)
304:    into v_items, v_locked, v_open_today
305:    from page p;
306:
307:    return jsonb_build_object(
308:        'items', coalesce(v_items, '[]'::jsonb),
309:        'locked_count', coalesce(v_locked, 0),
310:        'open_today', coalesce(v_open_today, 0),
311:        'limit', v_lim
312:    );
313:end;
314:$$;

402:-- 7) RPC: emlakÃ§Ä± gruplarÄ± (AC-1, AC-10). YalnÄ±z kendi satÄ±rlarÄ±.
403:create or replace function get_my_wa_groups()
404:returns jsonb
405:language plpgsql
406:security definer
407:set search_path = public
408:as $$
409:declare
410:    v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
411:    v_status text;
412:    v_synced timestamptz;
413:    v_groups jsonb;
414:begin
415:    if v_uid is null then
416:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized');
417:    end if;
418:
419:    select c.status into v_status
420:    from wa_connections c
421:    where c.agent_id = v_uid;
422:
423:    if not found then
424:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'status', 'none', 'groups', '[]'::jsonb, 'max', 20);
425:    end if;
426:
427:    select max(g.updated_at) into v_synced
428:    from wa_groups g
429:    where g.agent_id = v_uid;
430:
431:    select coalesce(jsonb_agg(
432:        jsonb_build_object('jid', g.group_jid, 'name', g.name, 'selected', (s.group_jid is not null))
433:        order by g.name nulls last, g.group_jid
434:    ), '[]'::jsonb)
435:    into v_groups
436:    from wa_groups g
437:    left join wa_group_selection s
438:           on s.agent_id = g.agent_id and s.group_jid = g.group_jid
439:    where g.agent_id = v_uid;
440:
441:    return jsonb_build_object(
442:        'ok', true,
443:        'status', v_status,
444:        'groups', v_groups,
445:        'max', 20
446:    ) || case
447:            when v_status = 'connected' then jsonb_build_object('groups_synced_at', v_synced)
448:            else jsonb_build_object('readonly', true)
449:         end;
450:end;
451:$$;
452:

454:revoke execute on function get_my_wa_groups() from anon;
455:grant execute on function get_my_wa_groups() to authenticated;

457:-- 8) RPC: seÃ§imi tamamen deÄŸiÅŸtir (AC-2, AC-4, AC-S2, AC-S7, AC-S8). Tek transaction.
458:create or replace function set_my_wa_group_selection(p_group_jids text[])
459:returns jsonb
460:language plpgsql
461:security definer
462:set search_path = public
463:as $$
464:declare
465:    v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
466:    v_status text;
467:    v_jids text[];
468:    v_matched integer;
469:    v_added integer;
470:    v_removed integer;
471:begin
472:    if v_uid is null then
473:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized');
474:    end if;
475:
476:    if p_group_jids is null then
477:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'invalid_input');
478:    end if;
479:
480:    if coalesce(cardinality(p_group_jids), 0) > 20 then
481:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'too_many', 'max', 20);
482:    end if;
483:
484:    select c.status into v_status
485:    from wa_connections c
486:    where c.agent_id = v_uid;
487:
488:    if v_status is distinct from 'connected' then
489:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'not_connected');
490:    end if;
491:
492:    -- Eleman null ya da >200 karakter -> unknown_group (hiÃ§biri yazÄ±lmaz).
493:    if exists (
494:        select 1 from unnest(p_group_jids) j
495:        where j is null or char_length(j) > 200
496:    ) then
497:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unknown_group');
498:    end if;
499:
500:    v_jids := array(select distinct j from unnest(p_group_jids) j);
501:
502:    -- Her jid bu hesabÄ±n wa_groups kaydÄ±nda olmalÄ±; biri bile deÄŸilse hiÃ§biri yazÄ±lmaz.
503:    select count(*) into v_matched
504:    from wa_groups g
505:    where g.agent_id = v_uid
506:      and g.group_jid = any(v_jids);
507:
508:    if v_matched <> coalesce(cardinality(v_jids), 0) then
509:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unknown_group');
510:    end if;
511:
512:    select count(*) into v_added
513:    from unnest(v_jids) j
514:    where not exists (
515:        select 1 from wa_group_selection s
516:        where s.agent_id = v_uid and s.group_jid = j
517:    );
518:
519:    select count(*) into v_removed
520:    from wa_group_selection s
521:    where s.agent_id = v_uid
522:      and not (s.group_jid = any(v_jids));
523:
524:    delete from wa_group_selection where agent_id = v_uid;
525:
526:    insert into wa_group_selection (agent_id, group_jid)
527:    select v_uid, j from unnest(v_jids) j;
528:
529:    -- AC-S8: iÃ§eriksiz kayÄ±t (yalnÄ±z sayÄ±lar).
530:    if v_added + v_removed > 0 then
531:        insert into wa_connection_events (agent_id, attempt_id, from_status, to_status, error_code, requested_action, detail)
532:        values (v_uid, null, v_status, v_status, 'selection_changed', null,
533:                jsonb_build_object('added', v_added, 'removed', v_removed));
534:    end if;
535:
536:    return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'selected', coalesce(cardinality(v_jids), 0));
537:end;
538:$$;
539:
540:revoke all on function set_my_wa_group_selection(text[]) from public;
541:revoke execute on function set_my_wa_group_selection(text[]) from anon;
542:grant execute on function set_my_wa_group_selection(text[]) to authenticated;

545:create or replace function admin_set_threshold(p_threshold numeric)
546:returns jsonb
547:language plpgsql
548:security definer
549:set search_path = public
550:as $$
551:declare
552:    v_uid uuid := auth.uid();

554:    if v_uid is null then
555:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized');
556:    end if;
557:

559:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden');
560:    end if;

569:    set value = excluded.value,
570:        updated_by = excluded.updated_by,

583:create or replace function admin_get_threshold()

613:-- 9c) SayaÃ§ RPC'leri (operatÃ¶r gÃ¶rÃ¼nÃ¼rlÃ¼ÄŸÃ¼). sidecar_heartbeat.counters ÅŸekli:
614:--     { "orchestrator": { <anahtar>: n, ... }, "sidecar": { <anahtar>: n, ... } }
615:--     bump_sidecar_counter: tek satÄ±r atomik +1 (ON CONFLICT ... DO UPDATE; satÄ±r kilidi altÄ±nda
616:--     mevcut counters okunup yazÄ±lÄ±r). YalnÄ±z service_role (orchestrator ve ingest_personal_listing).
617:create or replace function bump_sidecar_counter(p_key text)
618:returns void
619:language plpgsql
620:security definer
621:set search_path = public
622:as $$
623:begin
624:    if p_key is null or p_key !~ '^[a-z_]{1,40}$' then
625:        raise exception 'invalid_counter_key';
626:    end if;
627:
628:    insert into sidecar_heartbeat (id, counters)
629:    values (1, jsonb_build_object('orchestrator', jsonb_build_object(p_key, 1)))
630:    on conflict (id) do update
631:    set counters = coalesce(sidecar_heartbeat.counters, '{}'::jsonb) || jsonb_build_object(
632:        'orchestrator',
633:        coalesce(sidecar_heartbeat.counters->'orchestrator', '{}'::jsonb) || jsonb_build_object(
634:            p_key,
635:            coalesce((sidecar_heartbeat.counters->'orchestrator'->>p_key)::bigint, 0) + 1
636:        )
637:    );
638:end;
639:$$;
640:
641:revoke all on function bump_sidecar_counter(text) from public, anon, authenticated;
642:
643:-- set_sidecar_counters: sidecar kendi sayaÃ§ nesnesini YALNIZ 'sidecar' alt anahtarÄ±na yazar;
644:-- 'orchestrator' alt nesnesi korunur (PostgREST upsert counters'Ä± ezmesin diye ayrÄ± RPC).
645:create or replace function set_sidecar_counters(p_counters jsonb)
646:returns void
647:language plpgsql
648:security definer
649:set search_path = public
650:as $$
651:begin
652:    if p_counters is null or jsonb_typeof(p_counters) <> 'object' then
653:        raise exception 'invalid_counters';
654:    end if;
655:
656:    insert into sidecar_heartbeat (id, counters)
657:    values (1, jsonb_build_object('sidecar', p_counters))
658:    on conflict (id) do update
659:    set counters = coalesce(sidecar_heartbeat.counters, '{}'::jsonb)
660:                   || jsonb_build_object('sidecar', p_counters);
661:end;
662:$$;
663:
664:revoke all on function set_sidecar_counters(jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated;
665:
666:-- 10) Service-role RPC: kiÅŸisel ilan giriÅŸi (AC-3, AC-5, AC-6, AC-11, AC-S3, AC-S4).
667:--     owner ASLA p_record'dan okunmaz; p_agent_id sidecar oturumunun sahibidir.
668:--     SeÃ§im otoritesi DB'dir (AC-5, AC-9): source_group bu agent'Ä±n wa_group_selection
669:--     kaydÄ±nda yoksa (seÃ§im kaldÄ±rÄ±lmÄ±ÅŸ / hiÃ§ seÃ§ilmemiÅŸ) hiÃ§bir ÅŸey yazÄ±lmaz -> 'unselected'.
670:--     DÃ¶nÃ¼ÅŸ: 'approved' | 'pending' | 'duplicate' | 'unselected'. Tek transaction: hata -> tÃ¼mÃ¼ geri alÄ±nÄ±r.
671:--     SonuÃ§ ve eÅŸik fallback'i sayaÃ§lara bu fonksiyon iÃ§inde, aynÄ± transaction'da yazÄ±lÄ±r.
672:create or replace function ingest_personal_listing(p_agent_id uuid, p_record jsonb)
673:returns text
674:language plpgsql
675:security definer
676:set search_path = public
677:as $$
678:declare
679:    v_value jsonb;
680:    v_threshold numeric := 0.70;
681:    v_fallback boolean := false;
682:    v_score numeric;
683:    v_approve boolean;
684:    v_parsed jsonb;
685:    v_raw_id uuid;
686:begin
687:    if p_agent_id is null or p_record is null or jsonb_typeof(p_record) <> 'object' then
688:        raise exception 'invalid_record';
689:    end if;
690:
691:    -- SeÃ§im kontrolÃ¼ (yazmadan Ã¶nce). source_group null ise eÅŸleÅŸme olmaz -> unselected.
692:    if not exists (
693:        select 1 from wa_group_selection ws
694:        where ws.agent_id = p_agent_id
695:          and ws.group_jid = p_record->>'source_group'
696:    ) then
697:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_unselected');
698:        return 'unselected';
699:    end if;
700:
701:    -- EÅŸik: satÄ±r yoksa / okunamazsa / geÃ§ersizse varsayÄ±lan 0.70 (threshold_fallback).
702:    select s.value into v_value
703:    from app_settings s
704:    where s.key = 'personal_auto_approve_threshold';
705:
706:    if not found then
707:        v_fallback := true;
708:    else
709:        begin
710:            v_threshold := (v_value #>> '{}')::numeric;
711:        exception when others then
712:            v_threshold := null;
713:        end;
714:    end if;
715:
716:    if v_threshold is null or v_threshold < 0 or v_threshold > 1 then
717:        v_threshold := 0.70;
718:        v_fallback := true;
719:    end if;
720:
721:    if v_fallback then
722:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('threshold_fallback');
723:    end if;
724:
725:    -- Skor: null / sayÄ± deÄŸil / 0-1 dÄ±ÅŸÄ± -> pending (kuyruk).
726:    v_score := case
727:        when p_record->>'quality_score' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (p_record->>'quality_score')::numeric
728:    end;
729:    if v_score is not null and (v_score < 0 or v_score > 1) then
730:        v_score := null;
731:    end if;
732:
733:    v_approve := v_score is not null and v_score >= v_threshold;
734:    v_parsed := p_record->'parsed';
735:
736:    begin
737:        insert into raw_listings (
738:            source, source_group, source_message_id, raw_text, parsed, fingerprint,
739:            quality_score, status, owner_agent_id, source_group_name, reviewed_at
740:        )
741:        values (
742:            'whatsapp_personal',
743:            p_record->>'source_group',
744:            case when p_record->>'source_message_id' is not null
745:                 then p_agent_id::text || ':' || (p_record->>'source_message_id')
746:            end,
747:            p_record->>'raw_text',
748:            v_parsed,
749:            p_record->>'fingerprint',
750:            v_score,
751:            case when v_approve then 'approved' else 'pending' end,
752:            p_agent_id,
753:            p_record->>'source_group_name',
754:            case when v_approve then now() end
755:        )
756:        returning id into v_raw_id;
757:    exception when unique_violation then
758:        -- AynÄ± emlakÃ§Ä±da aynÄ± fingerprint/mesaj: tek kayÄ±t kalÄ±r, yazma yok (AC-11).
759:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_duplicate');
760:        return 'duplicate';
761:    end;
762:
763:    if v_approve then
764:        insert into listings (
765:            raw_listing_id, title, price, currency, rooms, area_m2, city, district,
766:            neighborhood, description, source_group_name, owner_agent_id
767:        )
768:        values (
769:            v_raw_id,
770:            coalesce(v_parsed->>'title', trim(concat(v_parsed->>'rooms', ' ', v_parsed->>'district', ' ', v_parsed->>'city'))),
771:            (v_parsed->>'price')::numeric,
772:            coalesce(v_parsed->>'currency', 'TRY'),
773:            v_parsed->>'rooms',
774:            (v_parsed->>'area_m2')::numeric,
775:            v_parsed->>'city',
776:            v_parsed->>'district',
777:            v_parsed->>'neighborhood',
778:            v_parsed->>'description',
779:            p_record->>'source_group_name',
780:            p_agent_id
781:        );
782:        perform bump_sidecar_counter('personal_auto_approved');
783:        return 'approved';
784:    end if;
785:
786:    perform bump_sidecar_counter(
787:        case when v_score is null then 'personal_queued_noscore' else 'personal_queued' end
788:    );
789:    return 'pending';
790:end;
791:$$;
792:
793:revoke all on function ingest_personal_listing(uuid, jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated;

73:-- 4) Feed RPC'leri: 0020 hali (owner filtresi yok, scope yok).
74:create or replace function feed_listings(p_filters jsonb default '{}'::jsonb, p_page int default 1)
75:returns jsonb
76:language plpgsql
77:security definer
78:set search_path = public
79:as $$
80:declare
81:    v_has boolean;
82:    v_lim integer;
83:    v_filters jsonb := coalesce(p_filters, '{}'::jsonb);
84:    v_page integer := greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_page, 1), 500));
85:    v_il text := v_filters->>'il';
86:    v_ilce text := v_filters->>'ilce';
87:    v_grup text := v_filters->>'grup';
88:    v_oda text := v_filters->>'oda';
89:    v_fmin numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMin' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMin')::numeric end;
90:    v_fmax numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMax' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMax')::numeric end;
91:    v_has_price boolean := v_fmin is not null or v_fmax is not null;
92:    v_items jsonb;
93:    v_locked bigint;
94:    v_open_today bigint;
95:begin
96:    select a.has_access, a.lim into v_has, v_lim from feed_access() a;
97:
98:    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
99:        return jsonb_build_object(
100:            'items', '[]'::jsonb,
101:            'locked_count', 0,
102:            'limit', 0,
103:            'reason', 'no_subscription'
104:        );
105:    end if;
106:
107:    with ranked as (
108:        select
109:            l.id, l.title, l.price, l.currency, l.rooms, l.area_m2,
110:            l.city, l.district, l.source_group_name, l.created_at,
111:            row_number() over (
112:                partition by (l.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
113:                order by l.created_at desc, l.id desc
114:            ) as rn
115:        from listings l
116:    ),
117:    flagged as (
118:        select
119:            r.*,
120:            (v_lim is null or r.rn <= v_lim) as is_open,
121:            ((r.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
122:                = (now() at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date) as is_today
123:        from ranked r
124:    ),
125:    filtered as (
126:        select f.*
127:        from flagged f
128:        where (v_il is null or f.city = v_il)
129:          and (v_ilce is null or f.district = v_ilce)
130:          and (v_grup is null or f.source_group_name = v_grup)
131:          and (not v_has_price or (f.currency = 'TRY' and f.price is not null))
132:          and (v_fmin is null or f.price >= v_fmin)
133:          and (v_fmax is null or f.price <= v_fmax)
134:          and (
135:                v_oda is null
136:             or (v_oda = '5+' and f.rooms in ('5+1', '5+2', '6+1', '6+2', '7+1', '8+1'))
137:             or (v_oda <> '5+' and f.rooms = v_oda)
138:          )
139:    ),
140:    page as (
141:        select f.*
142:        from filtered f
143:        order by f.created_at desc, f.id desc
144:        limit 20 offset (v_page - 1) * 20
145:    )
146:    select
147:        coalesce(
148:            jsonb_agg(
149:                case
150:                    when p.is_open then jsonb_build_object(
151:                        'id', p.id,
152:                        'title', p.title,
153:                        'price', p.price,
154:                        'currency', p.currency,
155:                        'rooms', p.rooms,
156:                        'area_m2', p.area_m2,
157:                        'city', p.city,
158:                        'district', p.district,
159:                        'source_group_name', p.source_group_name,
160:                        'created_at', p.created_at,
161:                        'locked', false
162:                    )
163:                    else jsonb_build_object(
164:                        'id', p.id,
165:                        'city', p.city,
166:                        'district', p.district,
167:                        'created_at', p.created_at,
168:                        'locked', true
169:                    )
170:                end
171:                order by p.created_at desc, p.id desc
172:            ),
173:            '[]'::jsonb
174:        ),
175:        (select count(*) from filtered f2 where not f2.is_open),
176:        (select count(*) from flagged g where g.is_today and g.is_open)
177:    into v_items, v_locked, v_open_today
178:    from page p;
179:
180:    return jsonb_build_object(
181:        'items', coalesce(v_items, '[]'::jsonb),
182:        'locked_count', coalesce(v_locked, 0),
183:        'open_today', coalesce(v_open_today, 0),
184:        'limit', v_lim
185:    );
186:end;
187:$$;
188:
189:revoke all on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from public;
190:revoke execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from anon;
191:grant execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) to authenticated;

193:create or replace function feed_filter_options(p_il text default null)
194:returns jsonb
195:language plpgsql
196:security definer
197:set search_path = public
198:as $$
199:declare
200:    v_has boolean;
201:begin
202:    select a.has_access into v_has from feed_access() a;
203:
204:    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
205:        return jsonb_build_object('cities', '[]'::jsonb, 'districts', '[]'::jsonb, 'groups', '[]'::jsonb);
206:    end if;
207:
208:    return jsonb_build_object(
209:        'cities', (
210:            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
211:            from (select distinct city as v from listings where city is not null) s
212:        ),
213:        'districts', (
214:            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
215:            from (
216:                select distinct district as v from listings
217:                where district is not null
218:                  and (p_il is null or city = p_il)
219:            ) s
220:        ),
221:        'groups', (
222:            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
223:            from (select distinct source_group_name as v from listings where source_group_name is not null) s
224:        )
225:    );
226:end;
227:$$;

26:function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
27:  return typeof value === "string" ? value : null;

30:// YalnÄ±z bilinen alanlarÄ± kopyalar: qr yalnÄ±z status='qr' iken taÅŸÄ±nÄ±r (AC-S2).
31:function parseView(raw: unknown): WaStatusView {
32:  const r: RawRecord = raw !== null && typeof raw === "object" ? (raw as RawRecord) : {};
33:  switch (r.status) {
34:    case "pending":
35:      return { status: "pending" };
36:    case "qr": {
37:      const qr = asString(r.qr);
38:      // QR yoksa (sÃ¼re dolmuÅŸ ya da henÃ¼z yok) bekleme durumuna dÃ¼ÅŸÃ¼lÃ¼r; poll devam eder.
39:      if (qr === null) return { status: "pending" };
40:      return { status: "qr", qr, qr_expires_at: asString(r.qr_expires_at) };
41:    }
42:    case "connected":
43:      return { status: "connected", phone_masked: asString(r.phone_masked) };
44:    case "disconnected":
45:      return { status: "disconnected", error_code: asString(r.error_code) };
46:    case "logged_out":
47:      return { status: "logged_out" };
48:    case "error":
49:      return { status: "error", error_code: asString(r.error_code) };
50:    default:
51:      return { status: "none" };
52:  }
53:}

115:    if p_action is null or p_action not in ('connect', 'disconnect') then
116:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'invalid_action');
117:    end if;
118:
119:    if p_action = 'connect' then
120:        if not exists (
121:            select 1 from agents a
122:            where a.id = v_uid
123:              and a.wa_consent_accepted_at is not null
124:        ) then
125:            return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'consent_required');
126:        end if;
127:
128:        if not exists (
129:            select 1 from subscriptions s
130:            where s.agent_id = v_uid
131:              and s.status in ('active', 'trialing', 'past_due')
132:              and s.current_period_end > now()
133:        ) then
134:            return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'subscription_required');
135:        end if;
136:
137:        select count(*) into v_recent
138:        from wa_connection_events e
139:        where e.agent_id = v_uid
140:          and e.requested_action = 'connect'
141:          and e.to_status = 'pending'
142:          and e.created_at > now() - interval '10 minutes';
143:
144:        if v_recent >= 3 then
145:            return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'rate_limited');
146:        end if;
147:
148:        select * into v_row from wa_connections where agent_id = v_uid for update;
149:
150:        if v_row.status in ('pending', 'qr', 'connected') then
151:            return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'status', v_row.status);
152:        end if;
153:
154:        v_attempt := gen_random_uuid();
155:
156:        insert into wa_connections (
157:            agent_id, status, requested_action, requested_at, attempt_id,
158:            qr, qr_expires_at, qr_count, phone_masked, error_code, updated_at
159:        )
160:        values (
161:            v_uid, 'pending', 'connect', now(), v_attempt,
162:            null, null, 0, null, null, now()
163:        )
164:        on conflict (agent_id) do update
165:        set status = 'pending',
166:            requested_action = 'connect',
167:            requested_at = now(),
168:            attempt_id = excluded.attempt_id,
169:            qr = null,
170:            qr_expires_at = null,
171:            qr_count = 0,
172:            phone_masked = null,
173:            error_code = null,
174:            updated_at = now();
175:
176:        insert into wa_connection_events (agent_id, attempt_id, from_status, to_status, error_code, requested_action)
177:        values (v_uid, v_attempt, v_row.status, 'pending', null, 'connect');
178:
179:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'status', 'pending');
180:    end if;
181:
182:    -- disconnect
183:    select * into v_row from wa_connections where agent_id = v_uid for update;
184:
185:    if not found then
186:        return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'status', 'none');
187:    end if;
188:
189:    update wa_connections
190:    set requested_action = 'disconnect',
191:        requested_at = now(),
192:        updated_at = now()
193:    where agent_id = v_uid;
194:
195:    insert into wa_connection_events (agent_id, attempt_id, from_status, to_status, error_code, requested_action)
196:    values (v_uid, v_row.attempt_id, v_row.status, 'pending', null, 'disconnect');
197:
198:    return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'status', 'pending');
199:end;

1:// EML-105 / AC-S6: Sidecar'Ä±n Supabase (PostgREST) istemcisi. Yeni baÄŸÄ±mlÄ±lÄ±k yok: global fetch.
2:// Yazma kapsamÄ±: wa_connections (yalnÄ±z ALLOWED_UPDATE_FIELDS), wa_connection_events,
3:// wa_connections silme, sidecar_heartbeat. BaÅŸka tabloya yazmaz.
4:// Her agent sorgusu agent_id eq filtreli; istek alma sorgusu yalnÄ±z requested_action dolu satÄ±rlar.
5:const { isValidAgentId } = require('./paths');
6:
7:const ALLOWED_UPDATE_FIELDS = new Set([
8:  'status',
9:  'qr',
10:  'qr_expires_at',
11:  'qr_count',
12:  'phone_masked',
13:  'error_code',
14:  'last_seen_at',
15:  'requested_action',
16:  'updated_at',
17:]);
18:
19:const ACTIVE_STATUSES = new Set(['active', 'trialing', 'past_due']);
20:
21:class SupabaseRest {
22:  constructor({ url, serviceKey, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, now = Date.now }) {
23:    if (!url || !serviceKey) throw new Error('SUPABASE_URL ve SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY gerekli');
24:    this.base = `${String(url).replace(/\/+$/, '')}/rest/v1`;
25:    this.serviceKey = serviceKey;
26:    this.fetch = fetchImpl;
27:    this.now = now;
28:  }
29:
30:  _headers(extra = {}) {
31:    return {
32:      apikey: this.serviceKey,
33:      Authorization: `Bearer ${this.serviceKey}`,
34:      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
35:      ...extra,
36:    };
37:  }
38:
39:  async _request(method, pathAndQuery, { body, prefer } = {}) {
40:    const res = await this.fetch(`${this.base}/${pathAndQuery}`, {
41:      method,
42:      headers: this._headers(prefer ? { Prefer: prefer } : {}),
43:      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
44:    });
45:    if (!res.ok) {
46:      // Hata metninde anahtar/gÃ¶vde yok; yalnÄ±z yÃ¶ntem, tablo yolu ve HTTP kodu.
47:      throw new Error(`supabase ${method} ${pathAndQuery.split('?')[0]} -> ${res.status}`);
48:    }
49:    if (res.status === 204) return null;
50:    const text = await res.text();
51:    return text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
52:  }
53:
54:  _agentFilter(agentId) {
55:    if (!isValidAgentId(agentId)) throw new Error('geÃ§ersiz agent_id');
56:    return `agent_id=eq.${agentId}`;
57:  }
58:
59:  // Bekleyen connect/disconnect istekleri (poll).
60:  async fetchPendingRequests() {
61:    const rows = await this._request(
62:      'GET',
63:      'wa_connections?select=agent_id,requested_action,attempt_id&requested_action=not.is.null',
64:    );
65:    return Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
66:  }
67:
68:  // YalnÄ±z izinli durum alanlarÄ± yazÄ±lÄ±r; diÄŸer alan denenirse hata.
69:  async update(agentId, fields) {
70:    const filter = this._agentFilter(agentId);
71:    const keys = Object.keys(fields || {});
72:    const disallowed = keys.filter((k) => !ALLOWED_UPDATE_FIELDS.has(k));
73:    if (disallowed.length > 0) throw new Error(`izinsiz alan: ${disallowed.join(',')}`);
74:    if (keys.length === 0) return;
75:    await this._request('PATCH', `wa_connections?${filter}`, {
76:      body: fields,
77:      prefer: 'return=minimal',
78:    });
79:  }
80:
81:  async insertEvent(agentId, ev) {
82:    if (!isValidAgentId(agentId)) throw new Error('geÃ§ersiz agent_id');
83:    await this._request('POST', 'wa_connection_events', {
84:      body: {
85:        agent_id: agentId,
86:        attempt_id: ev.attempt_id ?? null,
87:        from_status: ev.from_status ?? null,
88:        to_status: ev.to_status ?? null,
89:        error_code: ev.error_code ?? null,
90:      },
91:      prefer: 'return=minimal',
92:    });
93:  }
94:
95:  async deleteConnection(agentId) {
96:    const filter = this._agentFilter(agentId);
97:    await this._request('DELETE', `wa_connections?${filter}`, { prefer: 'return=minimal' });
98:  }
99:
100:  // Tek satÄ±r (id=1) upsert: yalnÄ±z zaman, oturum sayÄ±sÄ±, bayrak. counters BU upsert'te gÃ¶nderilmez
101:  // (PostgREST upsert counters kolonunu komple ezer ve 'orchestrator' alt nesnesini silerdi).
102:  // SayaÃ§lar ayrÄ± RPC ile yalnÄ±z 'sidecar' alt anahtarÄ±na yazÄ±lÄ±r (DB: counters || {sidecar: ...}).
103:  async heartbeat({ sessions_open, misconfigured, counters }) {
104:    await this._request('POST', 'sidecar_heartbeat?on_conflict=id', {
105:      body: {
106:        id: 1,
107:        last_beat_at: new Date(this.now()).toISOString(),
108:        sessions_open: Number.isInteger(sessions_open) ? sessions_open : 0,
109:        misconfigured: misconfigured === true,
110:      },
111:      prefer: 'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal',
112:    });
113:    await this._request('POST', 'rpc/set_sidecar_counters', {
114:      body: { p_counters: counters && typeof counters === 'object' ? counters : {} },
115:      prefer: 'return=minimal',
116:    });
117:  }
118:
119:  // Abonelik eriÅŸimi: active/trialing/past_due ve dÃ¶nem sonu gelmemiÅŸ (AC-9).
120:  async isSubscriptionActive(agentId) {
121:    const filter = this._agentFilter(agentId);
122:    const rows = await this._request('GET', `subscriptions?select=status,current_period_end&${filter}`);
123:    const nowMs = this.now();
124:    return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).some(
125:      (r) => ACTIVE_STATUSES.has(r.status) && Date.parse(r.current_period_end) > nowMs,
126:    );
127:  }
128:
129:  async listConnectedAgents() {
130:    const rows = await this._request('GET', 'wa_connections?select=agent_id&status=eq.connected');
131:    return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).map((r) => r.agent_id).filter(isValidAgentId);
132:  }
133:}
134:
135:module.exports = { SupabaseRest, ALLOWED_UPDATE_FIELDS };
